# Linode API Documentation

> Programmatically create Linode instances, deploy Kubernetes clusters, and use the full range of Akamai cloud computing products and services.

## API Reference: Administration

- [Administration index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/administration/llms.txt): full category index
- [Get your account](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-account.md): Returns the contact and billing information related to your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_account` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account view `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update your account](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-account.md): Updates contact and billing information related to your account. If you exclude any properties from the request, the operation leaves them unchanged.  > 📘 > > When updating an account's `country` to `US`, you'll get an error if the account's `zip` is not a valid US zip code.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature:  - You can't change the company for a parent account.  - Child account users can't run this operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_account` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account update \   --address_1 "123 Main St." \   --address_2 "Suite 101" \   --city Philadelphia \   --company My Company \ LLC \   --country US \   --email jsmith@mycompany.com \   --first_name John \   --last_name Smith \   --phone 555-555-1212 \   --state PA \   --tax_id ATU99999999 \   --zip 19102 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get available services for a region](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-account-availability.md): View the available services for your account, in a specific region.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_region_available_service` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account get-account-availability us-east `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete your account](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-cancel-account.md): Deletes an active account. Akamai attempts to charge the credit card on file for any remaining balance. An error occurs if this charge fails.  > 🚧 > > This operation permanently deletes your account, which _can't_ be recovered. Also, there is no warning prompt after you execute this operation.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, you need to contact your Akamai account team to delete a child or parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `cancel_account` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account cancel \   --comments "I'm consolidating my accounts" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List available services](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-availability.md): Returns a paginated list of the services available to you, for all Akamai Cloud Computing regions.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_available_services` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account get-availability `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get account settings](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-account-settings.md): Returns information related to your Account settings: Managed service subscription, interface settings for new Linodes, Longview subscription, and Network Helper.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_account_settings` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account settings `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Enable Linode Managed](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-enable-account-managed.md): Enables Linode Managed for the entire account and sends a welcome email to the account's associated email address. Linode Managed can monitor any service or software stack reachable over TCP or HTTP. See our [Linode Managed guide](https://www.linode.com/docs/guides/linode-managed/) to learn more.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `enable_managed` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account enable-managed `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update account settings](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-account-settings.md): Updates your account settings. For a Longview subscription plan, see [Update a Longview plan](/reference/put-longview-plan).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_account_settings` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account settings-update \   --network_helper false `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Acknowledge agreements](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-account-agreements.md): Accept required agreements by setting them to `true`. This remains until the content of the agreement changes. If it does, you need to run this operation again to accept it. If you set this to `false`, the API rejects the request and you need to open a [support ticket](/reference/post-ticket) to reset the agreement. Omitted agreements are left unchanged.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `acknowledge_account_agreement` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [List agreements](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-account-agreements.md): Returns all agreements and their acceptance status for your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_account_agreements` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`
- [Get network usage](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-transfer.md): Returns a Transfer object showing your network utilization, in GB, for the current month.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_network_usage` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account transfer `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Enroll in a Beta program](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-beta-program.md): Enroll your account in an active Beta program.  When running this operation, consider these points:  - To view active Beta programs, run the [List beta programs](/reference/get-beta-programs) operation.  - Active Beta programs may have a limited number of enrollments. If a program has reached its maximum number of enrollments, you'll get an error even though the request is successful.  - Beta programs with `"greenlight_only": true` can only be enrolled in by an accounts that participates in the [Greenlight](https://www.linode.com/green-light/) program.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `enroll_beta_program` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli betas enroll --id example_open `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List enrolled Beta programs](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-enrolled-beta-programs.md): Display all enrolled Beta programs for your account. This includes both inactive and active programs.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_enrolled_beta_programs` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli betas enrolled `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an enrolled Beta program](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-enrolled-beta-program.md): Display an enrolled Beta program for your account. The Beta program may be inactive.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_enrolled_beta_program` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli betas enrolled-view $betaId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List child accounts (Deprecated)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-child-accounts.md): **Deprecated** Returns a paginated list of child accounts you're part of the account delegations for.  > 🚧 Parent and child accounts feature in Identity and Access > > With the introduction of Identity and Access, the parent and child accounts feature has changed. This operation is now deprecated, so use the [List child accounts](/reference/get-iam-delegation-all-child-accounts) operation instead. To learn more about the changes, see [Changes to the parent and child accounts feature for migrated partners](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-child-migration-partner).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `child_account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli child-account list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a child account (Deprecated)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-child-account.md): **Deprecated** Returns details of a specific child account that you're part of the account delegation for.  > 🚧 Parent and child accounts feature in Identity and Access > > With the introduction of Identity and Access, the parent and child accounts feature has changed. This operation is now deprecated, so use the [Get a child account](/reference/get-delegation-profile-child-account) operation instead. To learn more about the changes to the parent and child accounts feature, see [Changes for migrated partners](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-child-migration-partner).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `child_account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli child-account view A1BC2DEF-34GH-567I-J890KLMN12O34P56 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a proxy user token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-child-account-token.md): **Deprecated** Creates a short-lived bearer token for a parent user on a specific child account.  > 🚧 Parent and child accounts feature in Identity and Access > > With the introduction of Identity and Access, the parent and child accounts feature has changed. This operation is no longer available. Use the [Create a delegate user token](/reference/post-iam-delegation-profile-child-account-token) operation instead. To learn more about the changes to the parent and child accounts feature, see [Changes for migrated partners](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-child-migration-partner).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `child_account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli child-account create A1BC2DEF-34GH-567I-J890KLMN12O34P56 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create an entity transfer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-entity-transfer.md): **Deprecated** Run the [Request a service transfer](/reference/post-service-transfer) operation instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [List entity transfers](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-entity-transfers.md): **Deprecated** Run the [List service transfers](/reference/get-service-transfers) operation instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`
- [Get an entity transfer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-entity-transfer.md): **Deprecated** Run the [Get a service transfer request](/reference/get-service-transfer) operation instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`
- [Cancel an entity transfer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-entity-transfer.md): **Deprecated** Run the [Cancel a service transfer](/reference/delete-service-transfer) operation instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [Accept an entity transfer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-accept-entity-transfer.md): **Deprecated** Run the [Accept a service transfer](/reference/post-accept-service-transfer) operation instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [List events](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-events.md): Returns a collection of event objects that represent actions you've taken on your account, over the last 90 days. The events returned depend on your user permissions.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_events` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `events:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli events list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an event](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-event.md): Returns a single event object.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_event` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `events:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli events view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Mark an event as seen](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-event-seen.md): Acknowledge an event by marking it as seen.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `mark_event_seen` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `events:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli events mark-seen 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List invoices](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-invoices.md): Returns a paginated list of invoices against your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_billing_invoices` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account invoices-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an invoice](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-invoice.md): Returns a single Invoice object.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. You can't interact directly with notifications, and a notification disappears when you've resolved its cause. For example, if you have an important ticket open, you can respond to that ticket to dismiss its notification.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_notifications` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account notifications-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create an OAuth client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-client.md): Creates an OAuth Client, which can be used to allow users (using their Linode account) to log in to your own application, and optionally grant your application some amount of access to their Linodes or other entities.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_oauth_client` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account client-create \   --label Test_Client_1 \   --redirect_uri https://example.org/callback `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List OAuth clients](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-clients.md): Returns a paginated list of OAuth Clients registered to your Account.  OAuth Clients allow users to log into applications you write or host using their Linode Account, and may allow them to grant some level of access to their Linodes or other entities to your application.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_oauth_clients` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account clients-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an OAuth client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-client.md): Returns information about a single OAuth client.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_oauth_client` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. - [Update an OAuth client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-client.md): Update information about an OAuth Client on your Account. This can be especially useful to update the `redirect_uri` of your client in the event that the callback URL changed in your application.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_oauth_client` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account client-update \   edc6790ea9db4d224c5c \   --label Test_Client_1 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete an OAuth client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-client.md): Deletes an OAuth Client registered with Linode. The Client ID and Client secret will no longer be accepted by [login.linode.com](https://login.linode.com), and all tokens issued to this client will be invalidated (meaning that if your application was using a token, it will no longer work).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_oauth_client` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account client-delete \   edc6790ea9db4d224c5c `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Reset an OAuth client secret](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-reset-client-secret.md): Resets the OAuth Client secret for a client you own, and returns the OAuth Client with the plaintext secret. This secret is not supposed to be publicly known or disclosed anywhere. This can be used to generate a new secret in case the one you have has been leaked, or to get a new secret if you lost the original. The old secret is expired immediately, and logins to your client with the old secret will fail.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `reset_oauth_client_secret` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account client-reset-secret \   edc6790ea9db4d224c5c `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add a payment method](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-payment-method.md): Adds a Payment Method to your Account with the option to set it as the default method.  - Adding a default Payment Method removes the default status from any other Payment Method.  - An Account can have up to 6 active Payment Methods.  - Up to 60 Payment Methods can be added each day.  - Prior to adding a Payment Method, ensure that your billing address information is up-to-date with a valid `zip` by running the [Update your account](/reference/put-account) operation.  - A `payment_method_add` event is generated when a payment is successfully submitted.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, this operation isn't available for child account users.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_payment_method` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli payment-methods add \   --type credit_card \   --is_default true \   --data.card_number 4111111111111111 \   --data.expiry_month 11 \   --data.expiry_year 2020 \   --data.cvv 111 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List payment methods](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-payment-methods.md): Returns a paginated list of Payment Methods for this Account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_payment_methods` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli payment-methods list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a payment method](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-payment-method.md): View the details of the specified Payment Method.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. - [Delete a payment method](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-payment-method.md): Deactivate the specified Payment Method.  The default Payment Method can not be deleted. To add a new default Payment Method, run the [Add a payment method](/reference/post-payment-method) operation. To designate an existing Payment Method as the default method, run the [Set a default payment method](/reference/post-make-payment-method-default) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. - [Set a default payment method](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-make-payment-method-default.md): Make the specified Payment Method the default method for automatically processing payments. Removes the default status from any other Payment Method.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, this operation isn't available for child account users.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `set_default_payment_method` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli payment-methods default 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add or edit a credit card](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-credit-card.md): **Deprecated** Please run [Add a payment method](/reference/post-payment-method). Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [Make a payment](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-payment.md): Makes a payment to your account.  - The requested amount is charged to the default payment method if no `payment_method_id` is specified.  - A `payment_submitted` event is generated when a payment is successfully submitted.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, this operation isn't available for child account users.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `make_billing_payment` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account payment-create \   --usd 120.50 \   --payment_method_id 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List payments](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-payments.md): Returns a paginated list of payments made on this Account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account payments-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Stage a PayPal payment](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-pay-pal-payment.md): **Deprecated** This operation is disabled and no longer accessible. See [Manage Payment Methods](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/platform/billing/guides/payment-methods/).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [Execute a PayPal payment](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-execute-pay-pal-payment.md): **Deprecated** This operation is disabled and no longer accessible. See [Manage Payment Methods](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/platform/billing/guides/payment-methods/).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [Get a payment](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-payment.md): Returns information about a specific Payment.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_billing_payment` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account payment-view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add a promo credit](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-promo-credit.md): Adds an expiring Promo Credit to your account. The following restrictions apply:  - Your account needs to be less than 90 days old.  - You can't already have a Promo Credit on your account.  - The `promo_code` needs to be valid and unexpired.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, this operation isn't available for child account users.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_promo_code` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli account \   promo-add \   --promo-code abcdefABCDEF1234567890 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Request a service transfer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-service-transfer.md): Creates a transfer request for one or more services you specify. A request can contain any of the eligible service types and any number of each service type. Currently, you can only transfer your Linode instances.  **Create the service transfer**  DNS records assigned to requested services _aren't_ transferred or updated. Ensure that you update any applicable DNS records or communicate them to the recipient before transfer.  When creating a service transfer, consider these points:  - Your account can't have a past due balance or other limitations. It also can't have any active terms of service violations.  - On your account, you need to have access to any transferred service.  - The service can't be assigned to another pending service transfer request or a request that's been [accepted](/reference/post-accept-service-transfer), but is incomplete.  - Linodes _can't_:      - be assigned to a NodeBalancer, Firewall, VLAN, or Managed Service.      - have a Reserved IPv4 address assigned to them. To transfer a Linode with a reserved IP, you must first convert the address to an ephemeral IP or unassign it.      - have any attached Block Storage volumes.      - have any shared IP addresses.      - have any assigned `/32`, `/56`, `/64`, or `/116` IPv6 ranges.      - have a [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set. You need to unlock the Linode by deleting the lock.  After you create a service transfer request, the email address assigned to your account will receive a message. The message also contains a transfer token you need to store, and instructions to complete the transfer.  > 📘 > > A requested transfer expires 24 hours after creation.  **Accept a service transfer**  You [accept](/reference/post-accept-service-transfer) a transfer using a separate operation. Once you do, the requested services are moved to the target account. Your Akamai Cloud services aren't interrupted due to a transfer. Backups for Linode instances are transferred as well.  A transfer can take up to three hours to complete once accepted. Once the transfer completes, billing for transferred services ends for your account, and begins for the account that accepted the request.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_service_transfer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli service-transfers \   create \   --entities.linodes 111 \   --entities.linodes 222 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List service transfers](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-service-transfers.md): Returns all [requested](/reference/post-service-transfer) and [accepted](/reference/post-accept-service-transfer) service transfers for this account, regardless of the user who requested or accepted the transfer.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_service_transfers` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli service-transfers \   list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a service transfer request](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-service-transfer.md): Returns details for the service transfer for the provided `token`.  - After a transfer has been [accepted](/reference/post-accept-service-transfer), it can only be viewed by users on the accounts that [requested](/reference/post-service-transfer) and [accepted](/reference/post-accept-service-transfer) the transfer and have the corresponding permissions.  - If [canceled](/reference/delete-service-transfer) or expired, only users of the account that requested the transfer can view it and have the required permission.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_service_transfer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli service-transfers \   view 123E4567-E89B-12D3-A456-426614174000 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Cancel a service transfer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-service-transfer.md): Cancels the service transfer for the provided `token`.  These points apply to canceling a service transfer:  - An cancellation notification is sent to the email address that's assigned to the account that requested the transfer.  - Once canceled, the transfer can't be accepted or acted on in any way. If you cancel by mistake, you need to [create](/reference/post-service-transfer) a new service transfer.  - You can't cancel a service transfer if it's expired or it's been [accepted](/reference/post-accept-service-transfer).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `cancel_service_transfer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli service-transfers \   cancel 123E4567-E89B-12D3-A456-426614174000 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Accept a service transfer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-accept-service-transfer.md): After you [request a service transfer](/reference/post-service-transfer) to another account, use this operation along with the `token` from the transfer request email, to accept it for the target account. Currently, you can only transfer your Linode instances.  After you accept a transfer, the email address assigned to your account will receive a message. The same message is sent to the email address assigned to the account that requested the transfer. Once the transfer completes, billing for transferred services ends for the account that made the request, and begins for your account.  There are several conditions that need to be met to accept a transfer request:  - Only transfers with a `pending` status can be accepted.  - To accept a transfer, your account needs to have a registered payment method, and it can't have a past due balance or other limitations. It also can't have any active terms of service violations.  - Any transferred service needs to be owned by the account that requested the transfer.  - Linodes _can't_:      - be assigned to a NodeBalancer, Firewall, VLAN, or Managed Service.      - have a Reserved IPv4 address assigned to them. The initiating account must convert the address to an ephemeral IP or unassign it before the transfer can be completed.      - have any attached Block Storage volumes.      - have any shared IP addresses.      - have any assigned /56, /64, or /116 IPv6 ranges.      - have a [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set. You need to unlock the Linode by deleting the lock.  > 📘 > > A requested transfer expires 24 hours after creation. You need to meet all of these conditions before this expiration to allow the transfer. Otherwise, you need to [request a service transfer](/reference/post-service-transfer) again.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `accept_service_transfer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli service-transfers \   accept 123E4567-E89B-12D3-A456-426614174000 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a user](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-user.md): Creates a user on your account. You determine the new user's account access by setting it to restricted or unrestricted:  - **Unrestricted access**. These users can access everything on an account.  - **Restricted access**. These users can only access entities or perform actions they've been given specific grants to.  After completion, the API sends a confirmation message containing password creation and login instructions to the user's `email` address.  > 🚧 RBAC Identity and Access > > If Identity and Access is enabled on your account, the _restricted_ user is assigned the `account_admin` role and for _restricted_ users you need follow this operation with [assigning roles to the user](/reference/put-iam-users-role-permissions). To learn more, see [Identity and Access for Akamai Cloud](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_user` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli users create \   --username example_user \   --email example_user@linode.com \   --restricted true `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List users](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-users.md): Returns a paginated list of all users on your account. A user can access all or part of an account based on their role assignment:  - **Unrestricted access**. These users can access everything on an account.  - **Restricted access**. These users can only access entities or perform actions they've been given specific grants to.  > 🚧 RBAC Identity and Access > > If Identity and Access is enabled on your account, the _restricted_ users have the `account_admin` role and _restricted_ users have [specific roles assigned to them](/reference/get-iam-users-role-permissions). To learn more, see [Identity and Access for Akamai Cloud](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_user` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli users list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a user](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-user.md): Returns information about a single user on your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_user` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli users view example_user `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a user](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-user.md): Update information about a user on your account, including its restricted status. When setting a user to `restricted`, the API sets no grants for it. You need to set grants so that user can access things on the account.  > 🚧 RBAC Identity and Access > > If Identity and Access is enabled on your account, the _restricted_ users have the `account_admin` role and _restricted_ users have specific roles assigned to them. To update the role assignment, run the [Update a user's access level](/reference/put-iam-users-role-permissions) operation instead. To learn more, see [Identity and Access for Akamai Cloud](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm).   **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, on a child account, you can't edit the `username` or `email` values for delegate users.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_user` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli users update example_user \   --username example_user \   --email example@linode.com \   --restricted true `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a user](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-user.md): Deletes a user. The API immediately logs the user out and removes their access to all Akamai Cloud services.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, you can't delete a user with the `delegate` user type on a child account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_user` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli users delete example_user `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List a user's grants](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-user-grants.md): **Deprecated** Returns the full grants structure for an account username you specify. This includes all entities on the account, and the level of access this user has to each of them. This doesn't apply to the account owner or the current authenticated user. You can run the [List grants](/reference/get-profile-grants) operation to view those grants.  > 🚧 RBAC Identity and Access > > Grants are now replaced with RBAC Identity and Access. Use the [Get a user's access level](/reference/get-iam-users-role-permissions) operation instead. To learn more, see [Identity and Access for Akamai Cloud](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_user_grants` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`
- [Update a user's grants](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-user-grants.md): **Deprecated** Update the grants for a [restricted](/reference/post-user) user. This can be used to give a user access to new entities or actions, or take access away. Omit a grant object from the request to keep its current setting.   > 🚧 RBAC Identity and Access > > Grants are now replaced with RBAC Identity and Access. Use the [Update a user's access level](/reference/put-iam-users-role-permissions) operation instead. To learn more, see [Identity and Access for Akamai Cloud](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_user_grants` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [Get an OAuth client's thumbnail](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-client-thumbnail.md): Returns the PNG thumbnail for this OAuth client.  This is a publicly viewable operation, and you can access it without authentication.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.
- [Update an OAuth client's thumbnail](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-client-thumbnail.md): Upload a thumbnail for a client you own. You need to upload a PNG image file that the Linode API returns when the thumbnail is retrieved. This image is publicly viewable.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_oauth_client_thumbnail` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`

## API Reference: Beta programs

- [Beta programs index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/beta-programs/llms.txt): full category index
- [List Beta programs](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-beta-programs.md): Display all active Beta programs. This operation can only be accessed by account [users](/reference/get-users) with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli betas list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Beta program](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-beta-program.md): Display information about a Beta program. You can use it to access inactive and active Beta programs. This operation can only be accessed by account [users](/reference/get-users) with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli betas view $betaId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Databases

- [Databases index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/databases/llms.txt): full category index
- [List Managed Databases engines](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-engines.md): Display all available Managed Databases engine types and versions. Use an engine's `id` to create a new Managed Databases instance.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases engines `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Managed Databases engine](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-engine.md): Display information for a single Managed Databases engine type and version. Run the [List Managed Databases engines](/reference/get-databases-engines) operation and store the `id` for the applicable database engine.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases engine-view `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Managed Databases](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-instances.md): Display information for all Managed Databases accessible to your user, regardless of engine type.  > 👍 > > For more detailed information, you can list Managed Databases, based on the `engine`, [MySQL](/reference/get-databases-mysql-instances), [PostgreSQL](/reference/get-databases-postgre-sql-instances), or [Valkey](/reference/get-databases-valkey-instances).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Managed Databases types](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-types.md): Display all Managed Databases node types. The type and number of nodes determine the resources and price of a Managed Databases instance. Each database can have one node type. With a high availability database, all nodes are deployed according to the chosen type.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases types `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Managed Databases type](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-type.md): Display the details of a single Managed Databases node type. The type and number of nodes determine the resources and price of a Managed Databases instance. Run the [List Managed Databases type](/reference/get-databases-types) operation and store the `id` for the applicable database node type.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases type-view `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create or restore a MySQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-mysql-instances.md): **Provision a MySQL Managed Database**  Use this operation to create a new MySQL Managed Database.  - New instances can take 10 to 15 minutes to deploy.  - When you create a new MySQL Managed Database, our partner [Aiven](https://aiven.io/docs/platform/concepts/cloud-security#data-encryption) automatically enables disk encryption on each cluster.  - All Managed Databases include automatic, daily backups. Up to seven backups are automatically stored for each Managed Database, providing restore points for each day of the past week.  - All Managed Databases include automatic updates, which apply security patches to the underlying operating system of the MySQL Managed Database. Configure the maintenance window for these updates with the [Update a managed MySQL database](/reference/put-databases-mysql-instance) operation.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. You should adjust the window to match a time that's the least disruptive to your application and users. Also consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - A successful request triggers a `database_create` [event](/reference/get-events).  - You can create a MySQL Managed Database in a VPC using the `private_network` object in the request.  > 📘 > > Currently, VPC subnets associated with Managed Database instances don't automatically block outbound connections outside the subnet. To limit network exposure, you should configure Cloud Firewall rules to explicitly deny outbound connections beyond the intended subnet. For more details on configuring rules, see the [Cloud Firewall](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/cloud-firewall) documentation.  **Restore a MySQL Managed Database**  Include the `fork` object in the request to target a backed-up database.  > 📘 > > Restoring from a backup creates a second running cluster, which incurs billing. Delete the first cluster after the restore is complete, to avoid this billing.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer an example API workflow you can follow to [restore a Managed Database backup](/reference/restore-a-managed-database-backup).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_database_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-create \   --label example-db1 \   --region us-east \   --type g6-dedicated-2 \   --cluster_size 3 \   --engine mysql/8.0.26 \   --engine_config.binlog_retention_period 600 \   --engine_config.mysql.connect_timeout 10 \   --engine_config.mysql.default_time_zone +03:00 \   --ssl_connection true \   --allow_list 203.0.113.1 \   --allow_list 192.0.1.0/24 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List MySQL Managed Databases](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-mysql-instances.md): Display all accessible MySQL Managed Databases.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a MySQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-mysql-instance.md): Display information for a single, accessible MySQL Managed Database.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a MySQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-databases-mysql-instance.md): Make changes to an existing MySQL Managed Database.  - The database's status can't be `suspended`.  - New values set in the `allow_list` overwrite existing values. To keep existing values, run the [List MySQL Managed Databases](/reference/get-databases-mysql-instances) operation, store the `allow_list` addresses from the response, and include them with any new addresses in this operation.  - Updates to your `allow_list` may take a short time to complete, making this operation inappropriate for rapid successive updates.  - Also allows resizing the database cluster to a larger one.  - All Managed Databases include automatic updates, which apply security patches to the underlying operating system of the Managed MySQL Database. Use the `updates` object in this operation to modify the maintenance window for these updates.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Use the `updates` object to adjust the window to match a time that's the least disruptive to your application and users. Also consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - You can't update `engine_config` advanced parameter settings for a suspended database. You'll need to [resume](/reference/resume-databases-mysql-instance) it first.  - A successful request triggers a `database_update` [event](/reference/get-events).  - You can update an existing MySQL Managed Database to move it to a Virtual Private Cloud (VPC) using the `private_network` object in the request.    > 📘   >   > Currently, VPC subnets associated with Managed Database instances don't automatically block outbound connections outside the subnet. To limit network exposure, you should configure Cloud Firewall rules to explicitly deny outbound connections beyond the intended subnet. For more details on configuring rules, see the [Cloud Firewall](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/cloud-firewall) documentation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-update 123 \   --label example-db \   --allow_list 203.0.113.1 \   --allow_list 192.0.1.0/24 \   --type g6-standard-1 \   --engine_config.binlog_retention_period 600 \   --engine_config.mysql.connect_timeout 10 \   --engine_config.mysql.default_time_zone +03:00 \   --updates.frequency weekly \   --updates.duration 3 \   --updates.hour_of_day 12 \   --updates.day_of_week 4 \ `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Patch a MySQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-mysql-instance-patch.md): Apply security patches and updates to the underlying operating system of the MySQL Managed Database. This function runs during regular maintenance windows, which you can configure with the [Update a managed MySQL database](/reference/put-databases-mysql-instance) operation.  - The database's status can't be `suspended`.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - A successful request triggers a `database_upgrade` [event](/reference/get-events).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-patch 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Suspend a MySQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/suspend-databases-mysql-instance.md): Suspend a MySQL Managed Database from your account, releasing idle resources and keeping only necessary data. All service data is lost if there are no backups available. This halts billing for the cluster.  - Akamai deletes suspended clusters after 180 days.  - A successful request triggers a `database_update` [event](/reference/get-events).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-suspend 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Resume a MySQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/resume-databases-mysql-instance.md): Resume a suspended MySQL Managed Database from your account. This resumes billing for the cluster.  - The database's status needs to be `suspended`.  - A successful request triggers a `database_resume` [event](/reference/get-events).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-resume 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a MySQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-databases-mysql-instance.md): Deletes a MySQL Managed Database from your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-delete 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases mysql-config `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**.
