- [Create or restore a PostgreSQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-postgre-sql-instances.md): **Provision a PostgreSQL Managed Database**  Use this operation to create a new PostgreSQL Managed Database.  - New instances can take 10 to 15 minutes to deploy.  - When you create a new PostgreSQL Managed Database, our partner [Aiven](https://aiven.io/docs/platform/concepts/cloud-security#data-encryption) automatically enables disk encryption on each cluster.  - All Managed Databases include automatic, daily backups. Up to seven backups are automatically stored for each Managed Database, providing restore points for each day of the past week.  - All Managed Databases include automatic updates, which apply security patches to the underlying operating system of the PostgreSQL Managed Database. Configure the maintenance window for these updates with the [Update a managed PostgreSQL database](/reference/put-databases-postgre-sql-instance) operation.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Adjust the window to match a time that's the least disruptive to your application and users. Also consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - A successful request triggers a `database_create` [event](/reference/get-events).  - You can create a PostgreSQL Managed Database in a VPC using the `private_network` object in the request.  > 📘 > > Currently, VPC subnets associated with Managed Database instances don't automatically block outbound connections outside the subnet. To limit network exposure, you should configure Cloud Firewall rules to explicitly deny outbound connections beyond the intended subnet. For more details on configuring rules, see the [Cloud Firewall](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/cloud-firewall) documentation.  **Restore a PostgreSQL Managed Database**  Include the `fork` object in the request to target a backed-up database.  > 📘 > > Restoring from a backup creates a second running cluster, which incurs billing. Delete the first cluster after the restore is complete, to avoid this billing.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer an example API workflow you can follow to [restore a Managed Database backup](/reference/restore-a-managed-database-backup).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_database_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-create \   --label example-db \   --region us-east \   --type g6-dedicated-2 \   --cluster_size 3 \   --engine postgresql/13.2 \   --engine_config.shared_buffers_percentage 41.5 \   --engine_config.pg.autovacuum_analyze_scale_factor 0.0 \   --engine_config.pg.autovacuum_vacuum_cost_delay 60 \   --engine_config.pg.pg_partman_bgw.interval 3600 \   --engine_config.pg.pg_partman_bgw.role myrolename \   --engine_config.pglookout.max_failover_replication_time_lag 60 \   --ssl_connection true \   --allow_list 203.0.113.1 \   --allow_list 192.0.1.0/24 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List PostgreSQL Managed Databases](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-postgre-sql-instances.md): Display all accessible PostgreSQL Managed Databases.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a PostgreSQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-postgre-sql-instance.md): Display information for a single, accessible PostgreSQL Managed Database.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a PostgreSQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-databases-postgre-sql-instance.md): Make changes to an existing PostgreSQL Managed Database.  - The database's status can't be `suspended`.  - New values set in the `allow_list` overwrite existing values. To keep existing values, run the [List PostgreSQL Managed Databases](/reference/get-databases-postgre-sql-instances) operation, store the `allow_list` addresses from the response, and include them with any new addresses in this operation.  - Updates to your `allow_list` may take a short period of time to complete, making this operation inappropriate for rapid successive updates.  - Also allows resizing the database cluster to a larger one.  - All Managed Databases include automatic updates, which apply security patches to the underlying operating system of the Managed PostgreSQL Database. Use the `updates` object in this operation to modify the maintenance window for these updates.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Use the `updates` object to adjust the window to match a time that's the least disruptive to your application and users. Also consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - You can't update `engine_config` advanced parameter settings for a suspended database. You'll need to [resume](/reference/resume-databases-postgre-sql-instance) it first.  - A successful request triggers a `database_update` [event](/reference/get-events).  - You can update an existing PostgreSQL Managed Database to move it to a Virtual Private Cloud (VPC) using the `private_network` object in the request.    > 📘   >   > Currently, VPC subnets associated with Managed Database instances don't automatically block outbound connections outside the subnet. To limit network exposure, you should configure Cloud Firewall rules to explicitly deny outbound connections beyond the intended subnet. For more details on configuring rules, see the [Cloud Firewall](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/cloud-firewall) documentation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-update 123 \   --label example-db \   --allow_list 203.0.113.1 \   --allow_list 192.0.1.0/24 \   --type g6-standard-1 \   --updates.frequency weekly \   --updates.duration 3 \   --updates.hour_of_day 12 \   --updates.day_of_week 4 \ `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Patch a PostgreSQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-postgre-sql-instance-patch.md): Apply security patches and updates to the underlying operating system of the PostgreSQL Managed Database. This function runs during regular maintenance windows, which you can configure with the [Update a managed PostgreSQL database](/reference/put-databases-postgre-sql-instance) operation.  - The database's status can't be `suspended`.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - A successful request triggers a `database_upgrade` [event](/reference/get-events).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-patch 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Suspend a PostgreSQL Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/suspend-databases-postgre-sql-instance.md): Suspend a PostgreSQL Managed Database from your account, releasing idle resources and keeping only necessary data. All service data is lost if there are no backups available. - [Create a PostgreSQL connection pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-postgre-sql-conn-pools.md): Configure PGBouncer connection pool settings, using our partner [Aiven's specification](https://aiven.io/docs/products/postgresql/reference/advanced-params#pgbouncer). You can only create a connection pool on an active cluster in an existing PostgreSQL Managed Database. Run the [List PostgreSQL Managed Databases](/reference/get-databases-postgre-sql-instances) operation, locate the desired database, and verify its `status` is not `suspended`. Then store both its `id`, as your `postgresqlInstanceId`, and its `label`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-conn-pool-create \   --database PostgreSQL-DB-video-streaming-data   --mode session   --label Connection-Pool-01 \   --size 10 \   --username john-doe `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List PostgreSQL connection pools](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-postgre-sql-conn-pools.md): View all of the PGBouncer connection pools for a specific PostgreSQL Managed Database. PGBouncer is an advanced configuration setting you can assign to your database, through our partner [Aiven](https://aiven.io/docs/products/postgresql/reference/advanced-params#pgbouncer).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-conn-pool-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a PostgreSQL connection pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-postgre-sql-conn-pool.md): Returns a specific connection pool for a PostgreSQL Managed Database.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli postgresql-conn-pool-view 890 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a PostgreSQL connection pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-databases-postgre-sql-conn-pool.md): Update PGBouncer connection pool settings for a PostgreSQL Managed Database, using our partner [Aiven's specification](https://aiven.io/docs/products/postgresql/reference/advanced-params#pgbouncer).  > 🚧 > > This operation can cause sudden closure of an in-use connection pool.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli databases postgresql-conn-pool-update 123 \   --database PostgreSQL-DB-video-streaming-data-legacy   --mode session   --label Connection-Pool-01 \   --size 10 \   --username john-doe `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a PostgreSQL connection pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-databases-postgre-sql-conn-pool.md): Delete a specific connection pool from a PostgreSQL Managed Database.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli postgresql-conn-pool-delete 890 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Valkey Managed Database advanced parameters](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-valkey-config.md): **Limited availability** Lists all advanced parameters you can apply to a Valkey Managed Database, via our partner [Aiven](https://aiven.io/docs/products/valkey/reference/advanced-params).  > 📘 > > Aiven may offer other parameters, but Akamai Managed Databases only supports the ones listed in this operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`
- [Create or restore a Valkey Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-valkey-instances.md): **Limited availability** Creates or restores a Valkey Managed Database.  - New instances can take 10 to 15 minutes to deploy.  - When you create a new Valkey Managed Database, our partner [Aiven](https://aiven.io/docs/platform/concepts/cloud-security#data-encryption) automatically enables disk encryption on each cluster.  - Valkey databases automatically back up data every 12 hours and support configurable data persistence using Redis Database Backup (RDB). To change the default 12 hours to 24 hours, set the `backup_hour` parameter in the `engine_config` object. It may take another backup cycle for the new backup time to be applied.  - All Managed Databases include automatic updates, which apply security patches to the underlying operating system of the Valkey Managed Database. Configure the maintenance window for these updates with the [Update a Valkey Managed Database](/reference/put-databases-valkey-instance) operation.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Adjust the window to match a time that's the least disruptive to your application and users. Also consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - A successful request triggers a `database_create` [event](/reference/get-events).  - You can create a Valkey Managed Database in a VPC using the `private_network` object in the request.  **Restore a Valkey Managed Database**  Include the `fork` object in the request to target a backed-up database.  > 📘 > > Restoring from a backup creates a second running cluster, which incurs billing. Delete the first cluster after the restore is complete, to avoid this billing.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_database_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`
- [List Valkey Managed Databases](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-valkey-instances.md): **Limited availability** Display all accessible Valkey Managed Databases.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`
- [Get a Valkey Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-valkey-instance.md): **Limited availability** Displays information for a single, accessible Valkey Managed Database.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`
- [Update a Valkey Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-databases-valkey-instance.md): **Limited availability** Updates an existing Valkey Managed Database. All values added in the request body overwrite the current cluster settings. Remaining values don't change.  - The database's status can't be `suspended`. This applies also to  `engine_config` advanced parameter settings for a suspended database. You need to [resume](/reference/resume-databases-valkey-instance) it first.  - Updates to your `allow_list` may take a short period of time to complete, making this operation inappropriate for rapid successive updates.  - You can resize the database cluster to a larger one.  - All Managed Databases include automatic updates, which apply security patches to the underlying operating system of the Managed Valkey Database. Use the `updates` object in this operation to modify the maintenance window for these updates.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Use the `updates` object to adjust the window to match a time that's the least disruptive to your application and users. Also consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - A successful request triggers a `database_update` [event](/reference/get-events).  - You can update an existing Valkey Managed Database to move it to a Virtual Private Cloud (VPC) using the `private_network` object in the request.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`
- [Delete a Valkey Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-databases-valkey-instance.md): **Limited availability** Deletes a Valkey Managed Database from your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`
- [Get Valkey Managed Database credentials](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-valkey-instance-credentials.md): **Limited availability** Displays the root username and password for an accessible Valkey Managed Database. The database's status can't be `suspended`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`
- [Reset Valkey Managed Database credentials](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-valkey-instance-credentials-reset.md): **Limited availability** Resets the root password for a Valkey Managed Database. A new root password is randomly generated and accessible with the [Get Valkey Managed Database credentials](/reference/get-databases-valkey-instance-credentials) operation.  - The database's status can't be `suspended`.  - It may take several seconds for credentials to reset.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`
- [Patch a Valkey Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-databases-valkey-instance-patch.md): **Limited availability** Applies security patches and updates to the underlying operating system of the Valkey Managed Database. This function runs during regular maintenance windows, which you can configure with the [Update a Valkey Managed Database](/reference/put-databases-valkey-instance) operation.  - The database's status can't be `suspended`.  - If your database cluster is configured with a single node, downtime occurs during maintenance updates. Consider upgrading to a [high availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/aiven-database-clusters#high-availability) plan to avoid any maintenance downtime.  - Major upgrades are optional until the service reaches end of service, and can be done in place.  - A successful request triggers a `database_upgrade` [event](/reference/get-events).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`
- [Resume a Valkey Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/resume-databases-valkey-instance.md): **Limited availability** Resumes a suspended Valkey Managed Database from your account. This resumes billing for the cluster.  - The database's status needs to be `suspended`.  - A successful request triggers a `database_resume` [event](/reference/get-events).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`
- [Get a Valkey Managed Database SSL certificate](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-databases-valkey-instance-ssl.md): **Limited availability** Displays the SSL CA certificate for an accessible Valkey Managed Database. The database's status can't be `suspended`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_only`
- [Suspend a Valkey Managed Database](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/suspend-databases-valkey-instance.md): **Limited availability** Suspends a Valkey Managed Database from your account, releasing idle resources and keeping only necessary data. All service data is lost if there are no backups available. This halts billing for the cluster.  - Akamai deletes suspended clusters after 180 days.  - A successful request triggers a `database_suspend` [event](/reference/get-events).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `database_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `databases:read_write`

## API Reference: Domains

- [Domains index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/domains/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a domain](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-domain.md): Adds a new domain to DNS Manager. You must own the domain before adding it here. Be sure to point your registrar to our nameservers. Akamai Cloud does not act as a registrar.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_domain_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains create \   --type master \   --domain example.org \   --soa_email admin@example.org `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List domains](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-domains.md): This is a collection of Domains that you have registered in Linode's DNS Manager.  Linode is not a registrar, and in order for these to work you must own the domains and point your registrar at Linode's nameservers.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Import a domain](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-import-domain.md): Imports a domain zone from a remote nameserver. Your nameserver must allow zone transfers (AXFR) from the following IPs:  - 96.126.114.97 - 96.126.114.98 - 2600:3c00::5e - 2600:3c00::5f   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains import --domain example.com --remote_nameserver examplenameserver.com `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a domain](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-domain.md): This is a single domain that you have registered in DNS Manager. For this domain record to resolve, you must own the domain and point your registrar at Akamai Cloud's nameservers. Akamai Cloud does not act as a registrar.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a domain](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-domain.md): Update information about a domain in DNS Manager.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains update 1234 \   --retry_sec 7200 \   --ttl_sec 300 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a domain](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-domain.md): Deletes a domain from DNS Manager. The domain will be removed from our nameservers shortly after this operation completes. This also deletes all associated domain records.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains delete 1234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Clone a domain](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-clone-domain.md): Clones a domain and all associated DNS records from a domain that is registered in DNS manager.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains clone 123 --domain example.com `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a domain record](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-domain-record.md): Adds a new domain record to the zonefile this domain represents.  Each domain can have up to 12,000 active records.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_domain_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains records-create 123 \   --type A \   --name test \   --target 203.0.113.1 \   --priority 50 \   --weight 50 \   --port 80 \   --ttl_sec 604800 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List domain records](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-domain-records.md): Returns a paginated list of Records configured on a Domain in Linode's DNS Manager.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains records-list 1234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a domain record](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-domain-record.md): View a single record on this domain.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains records-view 123 234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a domain record](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-domain-record.md): Updates a single record on this domain.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains records-update 123 234 \   --name test \   --target 203.0.113.1 \   --priority 50 \   --weight 50 \   --port 80 \   --ttl_sec 604800 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a domain record](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-domain-record.md): Deletes a Record on this Domain.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains records-delete 123 234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a domain zone file](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-domain-zone.md): Returns the zone file for the last rendered zone for the specified domain.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `domain_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `domains:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli domains zone-file 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Identity and access

- [Identity and access index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/identity-and-access/llms.txt): full category index
- [List entities](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-entities.md): Lists entities created on the account. An _entity_ is an object that has a life-cycle and can be managed by an authorized user within an account, for example: a specific virtual machine or firewall. You can manage an entity over time by performing operations on it, based on your access level.  > 📘 Before you begin > > Before you run this operation, read [Comparison of Linode API for grants and Identity and Access](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/migration-grants-identity-and-access#comparison-of-linode-api-for-grants-and-identity-and-access) to learn about the new experience and recommended use of the Identity and Access endpoints.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_entities`
- [List available roles](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-role-permissions.md): Lists available account access and entity access roles, their descriptions, and included permissions. Use these roles to manage users' access level to specific services and entities.  - **Account access roles**. These roles give access to account level operations, entities in the account both existing and to be created in the future, and creation of new entities.  - **Entity access roles**. These roles provide access to specific entities that are already available on the account.  To learn more, see the [Identity and Access in Cloud Manager terminology](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm#terminology).  > 📘 Before you begin > > Before you run this operation, read [Comparison of Linode API for grants and Identity and Access](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/migration-grants-identity-and-access#comparison-of-linode-api-for-grants-and-identity-and-access) to learn about the new experience and recommended use of the Identity and Access endpoints.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_role_permissions`
- [Get a user's access level](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-iam-users-role-permissions.md): Returns a user's access level on the account. The response includes account access and entity access roles.  - **Account access roles**. These roles give access to account level operations, entities in the account both existing and to be created in the future, and creation of new entities.  - **Entity access roles**. These roles provide access to specific entities that are already available on the account.  To learn more, see the [Identity and Access in Cloud Manager terminology](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm#terminology).  > 📘 Before you begin > > Before you run this operation, read [Comparison of Linode API for grants and Identity and Access](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/migration-grants-identity-and-access#comparison-of-linode-api-for-grants-and-identity-and-access) to learn about the new experience and recommended use of the new Identity and Access endpoints.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_user_permissions`
- [Update a user's access level](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-iam-users-role-permissions.md): Updates a user's access level on the account. The request includes account access and entity access roles.  - **Account access roles**. These roles give access to account level operations, entities in the account both existing and to be created in the future, and creation of new entities.  - **Entity access roles**. These roles provide access to specific entities that are already available on the account.  To learn more, see the [Identity and Access in Cloud Manager terminology](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-and-access-cm#terminology). This operation replaces any existing role assignment. To completely block user's access to the account, pass an empty list, `[]`. To get the list of available roles, run the [List available roles](/reference/get-role-permissions) operation.  > 📘 Before you begin > > Before you run this operation, read [Comparison of Linode API for grants and Identity and Access](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/migration-grants-identity-and-access#comparison-of-linode-api-for-grants-and-identity-and-access) to learn about the new experience and recommended use of the new Identity and Access endpoints.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_user_permissions`
- [Create an IDP configuration](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-idp-config.md): **Beta** Creates an external identity provider (IDP) configuration for the account. You can have only one configuration per account. To learn more about the single sign-on for Akamai Cloud, including emergency access accounts setup, see [Single Sign-On](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/single-sign-on-cm).  **Prerequisite**  You need to have IDP metadata from your identity provider.  To create the IDP configuration:  1. Provide a unique label for the configuration. 1. To activate the SSO, set `enabled` to `true`. 1. Enforce SSO for all users of your account with `enforce` set to `true`. The enforcement doesn't apply to users listed as [SSO user exceptions](/reference/get-idp-config-users-excluded).      > 📘     >     > Set the enforcement to `false`, if you want to test SSO on a small subset of users. After you create this configuration, [add the SSO-required users](/reference/put-idp-config-users-included) and test the login flow. If the test is successful, enforce the SSO login for all users with the [Update an IDP configuration](/reference/put-idp-config) operation.  1. Specify the SAML configuration settings with details from the IDP metadata you got from the identity provider.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer an example API [workflow](/reference/configure-sso-login) you can follow to create an IDP configuration, test it, and configure an account with emergency access.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_idp_config`
- [List IDP configurations](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-idp-configs.md): **Beta** Returns a list of all external identity provider (IDP) configurations for the account. For the initial release, the list contains only a single item.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_idp_config`
- [Get an IDP configuration](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-idp-config.md): **Beta** Returns the external identity provider (IDP) configuration for the account.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_idp_config`
- [Update an IDP configuration](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-idp-config.md): **Beta** Updates the external identity provider (IDP) configuration for the account. Top-level properties can be passed individually or together.  When included, you need to provide the entire IDP config object and it will overwrite existing values. This operation triggers an `update_idp_config` event.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_idp_config`
- [Delete an IDP configuration](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-idp-config.md): **Beta** Deletes the external identity provider (IDP) configuration for the account. The configuration object is soft-deleted, while certificates and user lists for both SSO-required or SSO exceptions are permanently deleted.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_idp_config`
- [Add an IDP certificate](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-idp-config-certificate.md): Adds a new public certificate to the external identity provider (IDP) configuration. One configuration can have several certificates to support a certificate rotation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_idp_config_cert`
- [List IDP certificates](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-idp-config-certificates.md): Returns the list of public certificates for the external identity provider (IDP) configuration. The IDP configuration needs to have at least one valid certificate at all times. You can [add several certificates](/reference/post-idp-config-certificate) or [remove the expired ones](/reference/delete-idp-config-certificate).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_idp_config_certs`
- [Delete an IDP certificate](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-idp-config-certificate.md): Deletes a specific certificate from the external identity provider (IDP) configuration. The IDP configuration needs to have at least one valid certificate at all times. If all existing certificates are expired, [add a new valid one](/reference/post-idp-config-certificate) before removing the expired ones.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_idp_config_cert`
- [List SSO user exceptions](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-idp-config-users-excluded.md): Returns the users excluded from SSO login. These users are part of the emergency account access (_break-glass user_) setup. They bypass the SSO enforcement and continue to log in with a password regardless if SSO is enabled or enforced for the whole account. To learn more, see [Emergency access accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/single-sign-on-cm#emergency-access-accounts).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_idp_config_user_excludes`
- [Update SSO user exceptions](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-idp-config-users-excluded.md): Replaces the list of SSO user exceptions. These users are part of the emergency account access (_break-glass user_) setup. They bypass the SSO enforcement and continue to log in with a password. Pass an empty `usernames` array to clear the list. To learn more about emergency access accounts and their recommended setup, see [Emergency access accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/single-sign-on-cm#emergency-access-accounts).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_idp_config_user_excludes`
- [List SSO-required users](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-idp-config-users-included.md): Returns the list of users who are required to authenticate with SSO when the SSO enforcement is disabled. These users can test the IDP configuration before enforcing it for everyone.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_idp_config_user_includes`
- [Update SSO-required users](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-idp-config-users-included.md): Replaces the list of users who are required to authenticate with SSO when the SSO enforcement is disabled. These users can test the IDP configuration before enforcing it for everyone. Pass an empty `usernames` array to clear the list.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_idp_config_user_includes`
- [List child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-iam-delegation-all-child-accounts.md): Returns the list of available child accounts under your parent account. With the `users` query parameter set to `true`, it also returns the account delegation for each child account. An _account delegation_ is a group of parent account users, either admin or non-admin, delegated to manage a specific child account. The child account's admins manage the role assignment of delegate users on the child account. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_all_child_accounts`
- [Get the account delegation for a child account](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-iam-delegation-child-account-users.md): Returns the list of users added to the account delegation for a specific child account. An _account delegation_ is a group of parent account users, either admin or non-admin, delegated to manage a specific child account. The child account's admins manage the role assignment of delegate users on the child account. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_delegate_users`
- [Update the account delegation for a child account](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-iam-delegation-child-account-users.md): Updates the list of users added to the account delegation for a specific child account. An _account delegation_ is a group of parent account users, either admin or non-admin, delegated to manage a specific child account. The child account's admins manage the role assignment of delegate users on the child account. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_delegate_users`
- [Get the default role assignment for delegate users](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-iam-delegation-default-role-permissions.md): Returns roles assigned to new delegate users by default.  A _delegate user_ is a user from the parent account delegated by a parent account's admin on the parent account to have access to your, child, account. Note that the delegate users' access can be modified just as a regular child account user, so the returned list doesn't provide the role assignment of all delegate users. To get the detailed role assignment of a delegate user, run the [Get a user's access level](/reference/get-iam-users-role-permissions) operation. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a child account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_default_delegate_access`
- [Update the default role assignment for delegate users](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-iam-delegation-default-role-permissions.md): Updates the default role assignment for new delegate users on your child account. A _delegate user_ is a user from the parent account delegated by a parent account's admin on the parent account to have access to your, child, account. Note that the change applies only to new delegate users coming to your account. To edit current delegate users' access, you need to run the [Update a user's access level](/reference/put-iam-users-role-permissions) operation. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a child account by its admin.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_default_delegate_access`
- [Get your account delegations](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-iam-delegation-profile-child-account.md): Returns the list of child accounts you are part of the account delegation for. An _account delegation_ is a group of parent account users, either admin or non-admin, delegated to manage a specific child account. The child account's admins manage the role assignment of delegate users on the child account. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_user_delegate_accounts`
- [Get a child account](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-delegation-profile-child-account.md): Returns details on a specific child account you are part of the account delegation team for. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_all_child_accounts`
- [Create a delegate user token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-iam-delegation-profile-child-account-token.md): Creates a short-lived bearer token for you as a delegate user to run API operations on a specific child account. The token inherits the permissions set by a child account admin for your delegate user. Your username as the delegate user follows the specific naming convention:      `<Delegate-Parent account usernaname>`-\<SHA256 hash of parent `company` name and child account `euuId`>  These variables only use the first 15 and 16 characters of these values, respectively.  > 📘 > > The API returns the raw token in the response. You can't get it again, so be sure to store it.  Example workflow:  1. [List child accounts](/reference/get-iam-delegation-all-child-accounts) and store the `euuid` for the applicable one. 2. Run this operation and store the `token` that's created for the delegate user. 3. Run operations on the child account using the stored `token` to authenticate API operations.  **Prerequisite**:  To manage a child account, you need to be added by a parent account admin to the _account delegation_ for the specific child account. An _account delegation_ is a group of parent account users, either admin or non-admin, delegated to manage a specific child account.     To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `No permission, the access is provided by adding a user to an account delegation`
- [Get user's account delegations](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-iam-delegation-user-child-accounts.md): Returns the list of child accounts a specific user is part of the account delegation for. An _account delegation_ is a group of parent account users, either admin or non-admin, delegated to manage a specific child account. The child account's admins manage the role assignment of delegate users on the child account. To learn more about the parent and child accounts feature and account delegations, see [Parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners).  > 📘 Parent and child accounts feature > > This operation is available only within the context of the parent and child accounts feature and can be run only on a parent account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_user_delegate_accounts`

## API Reference: Images

- [Images index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/images/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create an image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-image.md): Captures a private, gold image from a Linode disk and stores it for later use.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > This operation has specific requirements for use. Check out its [workflow](/reference/capture-an-image) for details.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_image` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write, linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli images create \   --label this_is_a_label \   --description "A longer description \     of the image" \   --disk_id 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List images](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-images.md): Returns a paginated list of images. An image can be one of two types:  - **Public image**. The `id` for these images begins with `linode/`. These images are generally available to all users. To limit the response to public images, don't include [authentication](/reference/get-started#authentication) when calling this operation.  - **Private image**. The `id` for these images begins with `private/`. These images are account-specific and only accessible to users with appropriate permissions. To view private images, you need to include authentication when calling this operation. The response includes both private and public images.  - **Shared image**. The `id` for these images begins with `shared/`. These are the images that are shared with the user.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `Public images are Internet facing and private images require the view_image permission` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli images list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Upload an image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-upload-image.md): Creates a new private image container and returns a URL as the `upload_to` object in the response. Use this URL to upload your own disk image to the container.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > This operation has specific requirements for use. Check out its [workflow](/reference/upload-an-image) for details.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `upload_image` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell # Run the operation to just get the upload_to URL linode-cli images upload \   --description "Optional details about the Image" \   --label "Example Image" \   --region us-east  # Upload the image file in a single step linode-cli image-upload \   --description "Optional details about the Image" \   --label "Example Image" \   --region us-east \   /path/to/image-file.img.gz `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-image.md): Get information about a single image. An image can be one of two types:  - **Public image**. The `id` for these images begins with `linode/`. These images are generally available to all users. To limit the response to public images, don't include [authentication](/reference/get-started#authentication) when calling this operation.  - **Private image**. The `id` for these images begins with `private/`. These images are account-specific and only accessible to users with appropriate permissions. To view private images, you need to include authentication when calling this operation. The response will also include public images.  - **Shared image**. The `id` for these images begins with `shared/`. These are the images that are shared with the user.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_image` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli images view linode/debian13 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update an image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-image.md): Updates a private image.  > 📘 > > You can't update the `regions` with this operation. Use the [Replicate an image](/reference/post-replicate-image) operation to modify the existing regions for your image.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_image` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli images update private/12345 \   --label "My gold image" \   --description "The detailed description \     of my image." `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete an image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-image.md): Deletes a private image.  > 🚧 > > - You can't undo this delete action. > > - When you delete an image, all [replicated instances](/reference/post-replicate-image) of that image are also deleted.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_image` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli images delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Replicate an image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-replicate-image.md): Target an existing image and replicate it to another compute region.  > 📘 > > As part of our limited promotional period, image replicas are free of charge until November 1, 2025. After this date, replicas will be subject to our standard monthly rate of $0.10/GB. When replicas become billable, your monthly charge will be calculated using the value returned in `total_size`, divided by 100. [Learn more](https://www.linode.com/blog/compute/image-service-improvements-akamai-cdn/).  - This is only available in a `region` that supports Object Storage, which stores the replicated image. Run the [List regions](/reference/get-regions) operation to review a region's `capabilities`.  - To replicate an image, it needs to have a `status` of `available`. Run the [List images](/reference/get-images) operation to view an image's `status`.  - To also keep the target image in its original compute region, you need to include that `region` in the request's data. If you leave it out, the API removes the image from that region. Run the [Get an image](/reference/get-image) operation to see the `regions` where an image currently exists.  - You can't include an empty array to delete all images. You need to provide at least one compute `region` where the image is `available`. Use the [Delete an image](/reference/delete-image) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `replicate_image` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli images replicate private/12345 \   --regions "us-mia" \   --regions "us-east" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-sharegroups.md): **Beta** Creates a group to share images with other users.  - Include existing `images` in the request or [Add images to a share group](/reference/post-sharegroup-images) later. Run the [Get an image](/reference/get-image) operation to see the existing images' `id` values. If needed, [Create an image](/reference/post-image) or [Upload an image](/reference/post-upload-image).  - You can set a `label` and `description` for the group and each image, visible to all group members.  - If omitted, the shared image keeps the original `label` and `description`. Note that when you [Update the original image](/reference/put-image), it has no effect on shared image's details within any groups.  - Run the [Add members to a share group](/reference/post-sharegroup-members) to include other users in the group. Use the group's `uuid` from the response to [Create a token](/reference/post-sharegroup-tokens) for user authentication.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups create --label "my_label" --description "my_description" \   --images '[{"id": "private/15", "label": "Linux Debian", "description": "Official Debian Linux image for server deployment"}]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List share groups](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroups.md): **Beta** Lists all owned groups with shared images.  > 📘 > > This operation doesn't list groups you're a member of. Run [Get a token's share group](/reference/get-sharegroup-by-token) to find these share groups using your membership token.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-sharegroup-tokens.md): **Beta** Creates a single-use membership token for users who want to join a share group with custom images.  - Before creating a token, get the share group's UUID from its owner. The owner can run [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) or [Get a share group](/reference/get-sharegroup) to get the group's UUID. If needed, [Create a share group](/reference/post-sharegroups) and share the UUID with the user.  - Share the `token` from the response with the share group owner. The owner can later use the token to [Add members to a share group](/reference/post-sharegroup-members) and manage the users' membership within the group.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned.
