[Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups token-create --label "my_token" --valid_for_sharegroup_uuid "abc123" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List a user's tokens](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-user-tokens.md): **Beta** Get details about all the user's tokens.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups tokens-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a token's share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroup-by-token.md): **Beta** Gets details about a share group you're a member of.  To get the `token_uuid`, run the [Get a token](/reference/get-sharegroup-token) or [Create a token](/reference/post-sharegroup-tokens) for the share group you want to join and share it with the group owner. You have to be accepted as a share group member before running this operation.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups view-by-token abc123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List images by token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroup-images-by-token.md): **Beta** Lists all the shared images a member can access within a share group, using a membership token. To get the `token_uuid`, run the [Get a token](/reference/get-sharegroup-token) operation.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups images-list-by-token abc123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroup.md): **Beta** Get information about an owned share group. Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `sharegroupId` path parameter that identifies an existing share group. If the `sharegroupId` in the request body belongs to a group you don't own, calling this API will result in a 404 error.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups view 1234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-sharegroup.md): **Beta** Updates the details of a share group you own. Run this operation to edit the `label` or `description` of a particular sharegroup.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  - You can update either of the fields, but when you provide a new `label`, make sure that it's not an empty string.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups update 1234 --label "new_label" --description "A new description." `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-sharegroup.md): **Beta** Deletes an owned share group. All shared group members lose access to the images within the group upon deletion.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups delete 1234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add images to a share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-sharegroup-images.md): **Beta** Adds images to an existing group where you can share them with other members.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  - Run the [Get an image](/reference/get-image) operation to see the existing images' `id` values. If needed, [Create an image](/reference/post-image) or [Upload an image](/reference/post-upload-image).  - You can set a `label` and `description` for each image, visible to all group members. If omitted, the shared image keeps the original image's details. Note that when you [Update the original image](/reference/put-image), it has no effect on a shared image's details within any groups.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups image-add 123 \   --images '[{"id": "private/15", "label": "Linux Debian", "description": "Official Debian Linux image for server deployment"}]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List shared images by group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroup-images.md): **Beta** Get the details about images shared in a particular group you own.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups images-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a shared image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-sharegroup-imageshare.md): **Beta** Updates the details of an image shared within a share group. Run this operation to edit the `label` and `description` of a particular shared image.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group. To get the `imageID`, run the [List shared images by group](/reference/get-sharegroup-images) operation.  - You can update either of the fields, but when you provide a new `label`, make sure that it's not an empty string.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups image-update 123 1234 --label "new_label" --description "A new description." `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Revoke access to a shared image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-sharegroup-imageshare.md): **Beta** Revokes access to a shared image within an owned group for all members.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group. To get the `imageID`, run the [List shared images by group](/reference/get-sharegroup-images) operation.  - To add a private image back to a share group, run the [Add images to a share group](/reference/post-sharegroup-images).  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups image-remove 123 1234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add members to a share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-sharegroup-members.md): **Beta** Adds users to existing shared private image groups as a group owner.  - Adding new members to share groups requires a single-use token. The user who wants to join a share group should run [Create a token](/reference/post-sharegroup-tokens) and share the `token` from the response with the group owner.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups member-add 123 --token "abc123" --label "my_sharegroup_member" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List members by share group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroup-members.md): **Beta** Get the details about all members in a particular share group you own.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups members-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a membership token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroup-member-token.md): **Beta** Get details about a membership token as a share group owner. Run this operation to check the `status` of a pending membership request or a current group member.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  - Get the `token_uuid` from the user who wants to join your share group or run the [List members by share group](/reference/get-sharegroup-members) operation for details on current group members.  - To get details about a token as a current or pending group member, run the [Get a token](/reference/get-sharegroup-token) operation.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups member-view 123 abc123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a membership token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-sharegroup-member-token.md): **Beta** Updates an existing membership token for a group owner. You can change each token's `label` that will be visible only to you, such as when you [Get members by share group](/reference/get-sharegroup-members).  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  - Run the [List members by share group](/reference/get-sharegroup-members) operation to get the `token_uuid` for a current group member.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups member-update 123 abc123 --label "new_label" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Revoke a membership token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-sharegroup-member-token.md): **Beta** Revokes a membership token accepted into a share group. Members with `revoked` status immediately lose access to shared images, and can't deploy them anymore.  - Run the [List share groups](/reference/get-sharegroups) operation to get the `id` you should use as the `(sharegroupId)` path parameter that identifies an existing share group.  - Run the [List members by share group](/reference/get-sharegroup-members) operation to get the `token_uuid` for a current group member. After revoking a membership, you can run this operation to check its status.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups member-delete 123 abc123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List share groups by image](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-images-sharegroups-image.md): **Beta** Lists all owned share groups where a given private image is currently shared. This operation returns an empty list for shared and distribution image IDs.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli images sharegroups-list private/12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-sharegroup-token.md): **Beta** Get details about a membership token as a share group member. Run this operation to check the `status` of a pending membership request or your current group membership. To get the `token_uuid` value, run the [List tokens](/reference/get-user-tokens) operation or [create a token](/reference/post-sharegroup-tokens) to generate one for the share group you want to join.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups token-view abc123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-sharegroup-token.md): **Beta** Updates the `label` of an existing membership token for a share group member or a pending membership.  To get the `token_uuid`, run the [List tokens](/reference/get-user-tokens) or [Create a token](/reference/post-sharegroup-tokens) operation to generate one for the share group you want to join.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups token-update abc123 --label "new_label" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-sharegroup-token.md): **Beta** Removes an existing membership token for a share group member or a pending membership.  - To get the `token_uuid`, run the [List tokens](/reference/get-user-tokens) operation.  > 📘 > > This operation is in Beta and available from the `v4beta` URL.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `images:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli image-sharegroups token-delete abc123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Linode instances

- [Linode instances index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/linode-instances/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-linode-instance.md): Creates a new Linode instance on your account. This incurs a charge on your account.  - There are several types of Linode you can choose from. Each provides a different size, processing potential, and cost. Run the [List Linode types](/reference/get-linode-types) operation for information on each available type.  - You can create a Linode in any available regions. Run the [List regions](/reference/get-regions) operation to see what's available.  - You need to provide at least one authentication mechanism for disk access: a `root_pass`, `authorized_keys`, or `authorized_users`, with the latter two offering SSH protection.  - To fight spam, Akamai restricts outbound connections on ports 25, 465, and 587 on all Linodes. For more information, see our guide on [Running a Mail Server](https://www.linode.com/docs/guides/running-a-mail-server/).  - To add or modify `tags` on a Linode, your account [user](/reference/get-users) needs `read-write` access for **Linode instances** and **Tags** operations. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.  There are several ways to create a Linode, using the objects available in this operation:  **Use an Image**  This includes using a Linode public image distribution or a private image that you created, based on another Linode.  > 👍 There are tutorials > > We offer example API workflows you can follow for both [public image distributions](/reference/create-a-linode-using-a-public-image) as well as [private images](/reference/create-a-linode-using-a-private-image).  **Use cloud-init**  Our cloud-init solution with [Metadata](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/compute/compute-instances/guides/metadata/) automates system configuration and software installation by providing a base-64 encoded [cloud-config](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/compute/compute-instances/guides/metadata-cloud-config/) file.  - You need a compatible image. To check for compatibility, run the [List images](/reference/get-images) operation and check for `cloud-init` under `capabilities`.  - This also requires a compatible region. Run the [List regions](/reference/get-regions) operation and look for `Metadata` under `capabilities` for a specific `region`.  **Use a StackScript**  You can use a StackScript to automate deployment of new systems, through a customized script.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer an example API workflow you can follow to create a new Linode [using a StackScript](/reference/create-a-linode-using-a-stackscript).  **Use a Linode backup**  You can create a backup of an existing Linode and restore it to a new one.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer an example API workflow you can follow to create a new Linode [using a backup](/reference/create-a-linode-using-a-backup).  **Create an empty Linode**  - Use this operation to create a new one.  - The Linode will remain `offline` and you need to manually start it. Run the [Boot a Linode](/reference/post-boot-linode-instance) operation.  - Manually create the Linode's [disks](/reference/post-add-linode-disk) and [configuration profiles](/reference/post-add-linode-config) (legacy) or [configuration profile interfaces](/reference/post-linode-config-interface).  > 📘 > > You should only use this method for advanced use cases.  **Linodes and interfaces**  Depending on your [account settings](/reference/get-account-settings), you can choose between legacy configuration interfaces or Linode interfaces when creating a Linode. Only one type of interface is allowed per Linode. The `interface_generation` field lets you select one interface type for new Linodes when both legacy and Linode interfaces options are available on your account. If a Linode is configured with a Linode interface, legacy configuration interfaces can no longer be used on that Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes create \   --label linode123 \   --root_pass aComplex@Password \   --booted true \   --stackscript_id 10079 \   --stackscript_data '{"gh_username": "linode"}' \   --region us-east \   --disk_encryption enabled\   --placement_group.id 528 \   --type g6-standard-2 \   --authorized_keys "ssh-rsa AAAA_valid_public_ssh_key_123456785== user@their-computer" \   --authorized_users "myUser" \   --authorized_users "secondaryUser" \   --metadata.user_data "I2Nsb3VkLWNvbmZpZw==" \   --firewall_id 9000 \   --ipv4 "192.0.2.141" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Linodes](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-instances.md): Returns all Linodes you have permission to view.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-instance.md): Get a specific Linode based on its unique `id`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-linode-instance.md): Updates a Linode.  > 📘 Updating `tags`? > > If you include the `tags` object in this operation to modify the Linode's tags, you need to include existing `tags` to maintain them. You can run the [List tagged objects](/reference/get-tagged-objects) operation to view your Linode, based on its `label` and store its current `tags`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes update 7833080 \   --label linode123 \   --backups.schedule.day "Saturday" \   --backups.schedule.window "W22" \   --alerts.cpu 180 \   --alerts.network_in 10 \   --alerts.network_out 10 \   --alerts.transfer_quota 80 \   --alerts.io 10000 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-linode-instance.md): Deletes a Linode.  > 🚧 > > Deleting a Linode is a destructive action and can't be undone.  Consider these points before deleting a Linode:    - Any unreserved (ephemeral) IP addresses assigned to the Linode are given up. Reserved IP addresses remain on your account and available to assign to another Linode or NodeBalancer within the same region. Reserved IP fees remain active as long as the address is held on your account, regardless of its assignment status.     - All disks, backups, configuration profiles, and interfaces assigned to the Linode are also are deleted.    - Any Block Storage volumes assigned to the Linode are detached.    - Billing for the Linode and its associated services stops. You're billed for time used within the billing period the Linode was active.    - You can't delete a Linode that's currently being [cloned](/reference/post-clone-linode-instance) or [restored from a backup](/reference/post-restore-backup).    - You can't delete a Linode that has a `cannot_delete` or `cannot_delete_with_subresources` [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set for it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes delete 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Boot a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-boot-linode-instance.md): Boots a Linode you have permission to modify.  If the Linode is using config profiles, and no parameters are given, a config profile is chosen for this boot based on the following criteria: - If there is only one config profile for this Linode, it will be used. - If there is more than one config profile, the last booted config will be used. - If there is more than one config profile and none were the last to be booted (because the Linode was never booted or the last booted config was deleted) an error will be returned.  If the Linode is using Linode interfaces, where `interface_generation` is set as `linode`, an error is returned if the Linode has to boot without any interface defined.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `boot_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes boot 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Clone a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-clone-linode-instance.md): Clone your Linode's existing disks, configuration profiles, and interfaces to another Linode on your account. Consider these points before cloning a Linode:  - Cloning to a new Linode incurs a charge on your account.  - If cloning to an existing Linode, any actions currently running or queued need to finish before you can clone to it.  - You can run up to five concurrent clone operations, from a single Linode. If you exceed this limit, you'll receive an HTTP 400 error.  - For Linodes using Linode interfaces, the region where you want the clone to live also needs to support Linode interfaces. You can run the [GET a region](/reference/get-region) operation to see what's supported. Your user's [account settings](/reference/get-account-settings) also need ot be set to allow creation of Linodes with Linode interfaces.  - Any [tags](/reference/get-tags) you have set on your source Linode will be cloned to the target Linode.  - If your source Linode is protected with a resource lock, the lock is _not included_ on the clone. You'll need to [add a new resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) if you want to protect the clone.  - If the target Linode uses Metadata, with `has_user_data` set to `true`, you need to create the clone with `metadata.user_data` in the request.  - If the target Linode has a `vpc` interface on its active legacy configuration profile, and it includes a `1:1 NAT`, the resulting clone is configured with an `any` 1:1 NAT. See the [VPC documentation](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/networking/vpc/#technical-specifications) guide for its specifications and limitations.  - Only next generation network (NGN) data centers (regions) support VLANs. If a VLAN is attached to your Linode and you try to clone it to a non-NGN region, the clone won't start. If a Linode can't be cloned because of an incompatibility, you're prompted to select a different region or contact support. See the [VLANs Overview](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/networking/vlans/#technical-specifications) guide for more specifications and limitations.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `clone_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes clone 123 \   --linode_id 124 \   --region us-east \   --type g6-standard-2 \   --label cloned-linode \   --backups_enabled true \   --placement_group.id 528 \   --disks 25674 \   --configs 23456 \   --private_ip true \   --metadata.user_data I2Nsb3VkLWNvbmZpZw== `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Migrate a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-migrate-linode-instance.md): Use this operation to move a Linode. There are two types of migration you can perform:  \* **Migrate to a new data center**. This applies when you want to move your Linode from one data center to another. You need to include the desired `region` for the new data center in the request. Run the [List regions](/reference/get-regions) operation to see all available regions and their feature capabilities. Also, see our [pricing page](https://www.akamai.com/cloud/pricing) for region-specific pricing, which applies after migration is complete.  \* **Initiate a pending host migration**. This applies to a migration that Akamai has scheduled for your Linode. Run the [List notifications](/reference/get-notifications) operation to see any pending host migrations. When the migration begins, the API shuts down the Linode if it's not already off. If the migration initiated the shutdown, the API reboots the Linode when completed.  Consider these points before starting any migration:  - You can't migrate a Linode to a new data center when that Linode has a `vpc` purpose, legacy configuration interface or a VPC Linode interface. Either of these situations can only be migrated within the same data center. See the [VPC documentation](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/networking/vpc/#technical-specifications) guide for its specifications and limitations.  - Only next generation network (NGN) data centers support VLANs. Run the [List regions](/reference/get-regions) operation to view the capabilities of each data center. If a VLAN is attached to your Linode and you try to migrate or clone it to a non-NGN data center, the operation won't initiate. If a Linode can't be migrated or cloned because of an incompatibility, you'll be prompted to select a different data center or contact support.  - NGN data centers don't support IPv6 `/116` pools or IP Failover. If you have these features enabled on your Linode and try to migrate to an NGN data center, the migration won't initiate. If a Linode can't be migrated because of an incompatibility, you'll be prompted to select a different data center or contact support. See the [VLANs Overview](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/networking/vlans/#technical-specifications) guide to view additional specifications and limitations.  - If the Linode is using a public Linode interface, the destination region needs to also support Linode interfaces. Run the [List regions](/reference/get-regions) operation to see their feature capabilities.  - After migrating to a different data center, Linode public interfaces retain the same number of IP addresses, but the IP addresses themselves change.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `migrate_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes migrate 123 \   --region us-central \   --placement_group.id 528 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Upgrade a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-mutate-linode-instance.md): Linodes created with now-deprecated Types are entitled to a free upgrade to the next generation. A mutating Linode will be allocated any new resources the upgraded Type provides, and will be subsequently restarted if it was currently running. If any actions are currently running or queued, those actions must be completed first before you can initiate a mutate.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `upgrade_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes upgrade 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Reset a Linode's root password](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-reset-linode-password.md): Resets the root password for this Linode.  - Your Linode must be [shut down](/reference/post-shutdown-linode-instance) for a password reset to complete. - If your Linode has more than one disk (not counting its swap disk), run the [Reset a disk root password](/reference/post-reset-disk-password) operation to update a specific disk's root password. - A `password_reset` event is generated when a root password reset is successful.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `password_reset_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes linode-reset-password 123 a$eCure4assw0rd!43v51 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Reboot a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-reboot-linode-instance.md): Reboots a Linode you have permission to modify. If any actions are currently running or queued, those actions must be completed first before you can initiate a reboot.  If the Linode is using Linode interfaces, where `interface_generation` is set as `linode`, an error is returned if the Linode has to reboot without any interface defined.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `reboot_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes reboot 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Rebuild a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-rebuild-linode-instance.md): Rebuild an existing Linode. A rebuild shuts down the Linode, deletes all of its disks and configuration profiles, then deploys a new `image` to the Linode with the given attributes. Consider these points before rebuilding a Linode:  - You need to supply an `image` for use in rebuilding the Linode. This can be a [private image](/reference/create-a-linode-using-a-private-image) you've created or uploaded, or a [public image](/reference/create-a-linode-using-a-public-image) we offer.  - You need to provide at least one authentication mechanism for disk access: a `root_pass`, `authorized_keys`, or `authorized_users`, with the latter two offering SSH protection.  - If your target Linode currently uses Metadata (`"has_user_data": true`), you should include `metadata.user_data` in the rebuild request to continue using the service.  - Linode interfaces don't change during a rebuild.  - During a rebuild, you can `enable` or `disable` local disk encryption. If disk encryption is not included in the request, the previous `disk_encryption` value is used. You can't disable disk encryption if the target Linode is attached to a LKE nodepool.  - You can resize the Linode to a different plan by including the `type` parameter, and setting it to a lesser plan. Consider the following points before resizing:    - You can't have an outstanding balance on your account.    - Resizing during a rebuild requires significantly more time.    - If you resize, the Linode will be migrated to a new hardware host, while rebuilding without resizing keeps the same hardware host.    - If any actions are currently running or queued for the Linode, you need to wait for them to complete.    - The Linode can't be pending migration.    - The Linode can't have more disk allocation than the new `type` supports. If this is the case, you need to delete or resize the disk to be smaller.    - You can [resize a Linode](/reference/post-resize-linode-instance) using a separate operation.    - If you have Block Storage volumes [attached](/reference/post-attach-volume) to the Linode, you can't resize it to a `type` that supports fewer volumes. You need to delete volumes from the Linode to meet the limit supported by the smaller `type`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `rebuild_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes rebuild 123 \   --image "linode/debian13" \   --root_pass aComplex@Password \   --disk_encryption disabled \   --authorized_keys "ssh-rsa AAAA_valid_public_ssh_key_123456785== user@their-computer" \   --authorized_users "myUsername" \   --authorized_users "secondaryUsername" \   --booted true \   --stackscript_id 10079 \   --stackscript_data '{"gh_username": "linode"}' \   --type "g6-standard-2" \   --metadata.userdata "I2Nsb3VkLWNvbmZpZw==" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Boot a Linode into rescue mode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-rescue-linode-instance.md): Rescue Mode is a safe environment for performing many system recovery and disk management tasks. Rescue Mode is based on the Finnix recovery distribution, a self-contained and bootable Linux distribution. You can also use Rescue Mode for tasks other than disaster recovery, such as formatting disks to use different file systems, copying data between disks, and downloading files from a disk via SSH and SFTP.  Linodes with legacy configuration interfaces receive a public IP and boot into the recovery Linux distribution. Linodes with Linode interfaces still boot into recovery mode with the recovery Linux distribution, but they retain their original network interfaces and settings from before entering rescue mode.  - Note that `sdh` is reserved and unavailable during rescue.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `rescue_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes rescue 123 \   --devices.sda.disk_id 124458 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Resize a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-resize-linode-instance.md): Resize a Linode to a different plan (`type`). Consider these points before resizing:  - You can't have an outstanding balance on your account.  - If any actions are currently running or queued for the Linode, you need to wait for them to complete before you can resize it.  - The Linode can't be pending migration.  - The Linode can't have more disk allocation than what the new `type` supports. If this is the case, you need to delete or resize the disk to be smaller.  - You can also resize a Linode when using the [Rebuild a Linode](/reference/post-rebuild-linode-instance) operation.  - If you have Block Storage volumes [attached](/reference/post-attach-volume) to the Linode, you can't resize it to a `type` that supports fewer volumes. You need to delete volumes from the Linode to meet the limit supported by the smaller `type`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `resize_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes resize 123 \   --type g6-standard-2 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Shut down a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-shutdown-linode-instance.md): Shuts down a Linode you have permission to modify. If any actions are currently running or queued, those actions must be completed first before you can initiate a shutdown.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `shutdown_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes shutdown 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List types](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-types.md): Returns Linode types, including pricing and specifications for each. Use these when [creating](/reference/post-linode-instance) or [resizing](/reference/post-resize-linode-instance) Linodes.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes types `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a type](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-type.md): Returns information about a specific Linode type, including pricing and specifications. This is used when [creating](/reference/post-linode-instance) or [resizing](/reference/post-resize-linode-instance) Linodes.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes type-view g6-standard-2 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Linode NodeBalancers](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-node-balancers.md): Returns a list of NodeBalancers that are assigned to this Linode and readable by the requesting User.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_linode_nodebalancers` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes nodebalancers 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List a Linode's volumes](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-volumes.md): View the Block Storage volumes attached to this Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_linode_volumes` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes volumes 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a snapshot](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-snapshot.md): Creates a snapshot backup of a Linode.  > 📘 > > - Backups aren't encrypted even when they're taken from an encrypted disk. When a backup is restored, and if encryption is enabled, the data stored on the disk is encrypted again. > > - If you already have a snapshot of this Linode, the previous snapshot will be deleted.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_linode_backup_snapshot` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes snapshot 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List backups](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-backups.md): Returns information about this Linode's available backups.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes backups-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Cancel backups](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-cancel-backups.md): Cancels the Backup service on the given Linode. Deletes all of this Linode's existing backups forever.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `cancel_linode_backups` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes backups-cancel 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Enable backups](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-enable-backups.md): Enables backups for the specified Linode.  > 📘 > > Backups aren't encrypted even when they're taken from an encrypted disk. When a backup is restored, and if encryption is enabled, the data stored on the disk is encrypted again.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `enable_linode_backups` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes backups-enable 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a backup](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-backup.md): Returns information about a Backup.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_backup` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes backup-view 123 123456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Restore a backup](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-restore-backup.md): Restores a Linode's backup to the specified Linode.  - Backups can't be restored across regions.  - You can only restore a successfully completed backup that is not under maintenance.  - The Linode that the backup is being restored to can't be the target of a current backup.  When you restore a backup, the restored disk is assigned the same [UUID](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Universally_unique_identifier) as the original disk. In most cases, this is acceptable and doesn't cause issues. However, if you try to mount both the original disk and the corresponding restore disk at the same time (by assigning them both to devices in your Configuration Profile's **Block Device Assignment**), you'll encounter a UUID "collision".  When this happens, the system selects, and mounts, only one of the disks at random. This is because both disks are sharing the same UUID. Your instance _may fail to boot_ because the API can't tell which disk is root. If your system boots in this scenario, you won't see an immediate indication if you're booted into the restored disk or the original disk, and you'll be unable to access both disks at the same time.  To avoid this, only restore a backup to the same Linode if you don't intend to mount them at the same time, or you're comfortable modifying UUIDs. If you need access to files on both the original disk and the restored disk simultaneously -- for example, if you need to copy files between them -- you should restore the backup to a separate Linode or [create](/reference/post-linode-instance) a new Linode using the desired `backup_id`.  To learn more about block device assignments and viewing your disks' UUIDs, see our guide on [Configuration Profiles](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/compute/compute-instances/guides/configuration-profiles/#block-device-assignment).  > 📘 > > Backups aren't encrypted even when they're taken from an encrypted disk. When a backup is restored, and if encryption is enabled, the data stored on the disk is encrypted again.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `restore_linode_backup` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes backup-restore 123 123456 \   --linode_id 234 \   --overwrite true `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a configuration profile](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-add-linode-config.md): Adds a new configuration profile to a Linode. Configuration profiles define the kernel, devices, and how much memory a Linode boots with.  > 📘 > > This operation is for legacy configuration profiles only, and not [Linode interfaces](/reference/post-linode-interface).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_linode_config_profile` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-create 7590910 \   --label "My Config" \   --devices.sda.disk_id 123456 \   --devices.sdb.disk_id 123457 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List configuration profiles](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-configs.md): Lists configuration profiles associated with a Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes configs-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a configuration profile](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-config.md): Returns information about a specific configuration profile.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_config_profile` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-view 123 23456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a configuration profile](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-linode-config.md): Updates a configuration profile.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode_config_profile` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-update 123 23456 \   --kernel "linode/latest-64bit" \   --comments "This is my main Config" \   --memory_limit 2048 \   --run_level default \   --virt_mode paravirt \   --helpers.updatedb_disabled true \   --helpers.distro true \   --helpers.modules_dep true \   --helpers.network true \   --helpers.devtmpfs_automount false \   --label "My Config" \   --devices.sda.disk_id 123456 \   --devices.sdb.disk_id 123457 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a configuration profile](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-linode-config.md): Delete a specific configuration profile from the specified Linode.  You can't delete a configuration profile from a Linode that has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set for it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_linode_config_profile` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-delete 123 23456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add a configuration profile interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-linode-config-interface.md): Creates and appends a single interface to the end of the `interfaces` array for an existing configuration profile. After you add the interface, you need to reboot the target Linode.  - A successful request triggers a `linode_config_update` [event](/reference/get-events).  - Only one interface can be set as `primary`. Setting `primary` to `true` for an interface sets all other interfaces to `false`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_linode_config_profile_interface` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-interface-add $linodeId $configId \   --purpose vpc \   --primary false \   --subnet_id 101 \   --ipv4.vpc "10.0.1.2" \   --ipv4.nat_1_1 "203.0.113.2" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List configuration profile interfaces](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-config-interfaces.md): Returns all configuration profile interfaces assigned to a specific configuration profile, on a specific Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_config_profile_interface` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-interfaces-list $linodeId $configId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Reorder configuration profile interfaces](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-linode-config-interfaces.md): Reorders the existing Interfaces of a Configuration Profile.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `reorder_linode_config_profile_interfaces` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-interfaces-order $linodeId $configId \   --ids 101 --ids 102 --ids 103 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a configuration profile interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-config-interface.md): Returns a single configuration profile interface.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_config_profile_interface` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-interface-view $linodeId $configId $interfaceId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a configuration profile interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-linode-config-interface.md): Update a `vpc` or `public` configuration profile interface for a specific configuration profile, on a specific Linode.  - A successful request triggers a `linode_config_update` [event](/reference/get-events).  - Only certain attributes can be updated for a configuration profile interface. You need to [add](/reference/post-linode-config-interface) a new configuration profile interface on your Linode if you need new values for any other attribute. Here are the supported objects, based on the interface's `purpose`:    - `public`. The `primary` attribute.    - `vpc`. The `ip_ranges`, `ipv4`, or `primary` attributes.  - You can't update a configuration profile with a `purpose` of `vlan`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode_config_profile_interface` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-interface-update $linodeId $configId $interfaceId \   --primary true \   --ipv4.vpc "10.0.1.2" \   --ipv4.nat_1_1 "203.0.113.2" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a configuration profile interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-linode-config-interface.md): Deletes a configuration profile interface from a specific configuration profile, on a specific Linode. A successful request triggers a `linode_config_update` [event](/reference/get-events).  Consider these points before deleting a configuration profile interface:  - You can't delete an active configuration profile interface. First, you need to shut down the associated Linode or restart it using another configuration profile.  - You can't delete a configuration profile interface from a Linode that has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set for it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_linode_config_profile_interface` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes config-interface-delete $linodeId $configId $interfaceId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a disk](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-add-linode-disk.md): Add a new disk to an existing Linode. You can create an empty disk to manually configure it later. You can also target a stored `image` to build the disk using a pre-configured file system--either through an image you've [created](/reference/post-image) or via a StackScript. Consider these points when creating a new disk:  **General**  - A Linode can have up to 50 disks.  - You need to provide at least one authentication mechanism for disk access: a `root_pass`, `authorized_keys`, or `authorized_users`, with the latter two offering SSH protection.  **Empty disks**  - To do this, don't include the `image`, `filesystem`, `stackscript_id`, or `stackscript_data` fields.  - An empty disk still requires a `label`.  - The default file system for an empty disk is `ext4`.  **Disks created from an `image`**  - A created disk inherits both the `label` and the `filesystem` from the target `image`, unless you specify otherwise.  - We offer an example workflow to [create a disk using a stored image](/reference/deploy-an-image).  **Disks created from a StackScript**  - You can run [List StackScripts](/reference/get-stack-scripts) to review available StackScripts.  - You need to include a StackScript-compatible `image` when creating the disk. Run [Get a StackScript](/reference/get-stack-script) to review available images.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_linode_disk` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes disk-create 123 \   --size 1300 \   --authorized_keys "ssh-rsa AAAA_valid_public_ssh_key_123456785== user@their-computer" \   --authorized_users "myUser" \   --authorized_users "secondaryUser" \   --root_pass aComplex@Password \   --image "linode/debian13" \   --stackscript_id 10079 \   --stackscript_data '{"gh_username": "linode"}' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List disks](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-disks.md): View information for disks associated with this Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_disk` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes disks-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a disk](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-disk.md): View information for a specific disk assigned to a Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_disk` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes disk-view 123 25674 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a disk](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-disk.md): Update a specific disk on a Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode_disk` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes disk-update 123 25674 \   --label "Debian 13 Disk" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a disk](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-disk.md): Delete a specific disk from a Linode.  > 🚧 > > Deleting a disk is a destructive action and can't be undone.  You can't delete a disk on a Linode that has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set for it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. - [Clone a disk](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-clone-linode-disk.md): Copies a disk, byte-for-byte, into a new disk on the same Linode. The operation fails if the target doesn't have enough storage space. A Linode can have up to 50 disks.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned.
