- [Reset a disk root password](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-reset-disk-password.md): Resets the password of a disk.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `reset_linode_disk_root_password` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes disk-reset-password \   123 25674 \   --password aComplex@Password `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Resize a disk](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-resize-disk.md): Resizes a Disk.  The Disk must not be in use. If the Disk is in use, the request will succeed but the resize will ultimately fail. For a request to succeed, the Linode must be shut down prior to resizing the Disk, or the Disk must not be assigned to the Linode's active Configuration Profile.  If you are resizing the Disk to a smaller size, it cannot be made smaller than what is required by the total size of the files current on the Disk.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `resize_linode_disk` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes disk-resize 123 25674 \   --size 2048 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List a Linode's firewalls](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-firewalls.md): View Firewall information for Firewalls assigned to this Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_linode_firewalls` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes firewalls-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a Linode's firewalls](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-linode-firewalls.md): Replace the current list of assigned firewalls with a new list, or provide an empty list to remove all firewalls from this Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode_firewalls` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes firewalls-update 123 \   --firewall_ids '[1234]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Apply a Linode's firewalls](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-apply-firewalls.md): Reapply assigned firewalls to a Linode in case they were not applied successfully.  The `firewall_apply` event indicates if the firewalls were applied.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `apply_linode_firewalls` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes apply-firewalls 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add a Linode interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-linode-interface.md): Creates an interface for the Linode. This interface links to the Linode, rather than to a configuration profile. You can create, delete, or update interfaces when the Linode is either powered off or in the process of being created.  Firewalls are applied to the Linode's VPC or public interface, rather than directly on the Linode. You can add, delete, or update these firewalls at any time. Firewalls are not applied to VLAN interfaces.  A successful request triggers an `interface_create` [event](/reference/get-events).  You need to set one interface type: `vpc`, `public`, or `vlan`. Omit the others or set them to `null`.  A Linode can have up to three interfaces:  - Only one `public` interface is allowed on a Linode.  - Multiple `vlan` interfaces are allowed, provided that they belong to distinct VLANs, which have unique `vlan_labels`.  - One `vpc` interface is allowed.  The Linode must be located in a region that supports Linode interfaces. Run [Get a region](/reference/get-region) to see if interfaces are supported in that region.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  _Public interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-add $linodeId \   --firewall_id 123 \   --default_route.ipv4 true \   --default_route.ipv6 true \   --public.ipv4.addresses '[{"address": "192.0.2.141", "primary": true}, {"address": "auto", "primary": false}]' \   --public.ipv6.ranges '[{"range": "2001:0db8::1/64"}, {"range": "/64"}]' `  _VLAN interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-add $linodeId \   --vlan.vlan_label my-vlan \   --vlan.ipam_address 192.168.2.2/24 `  _VPC interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-add $linodeId \   --firewall_id 123 \   --default_route.ipv4 true \   --vpc.subnet_id 321 \   --vpc.ipv4.addresses '[{"address": "10.0.0.1", "primary": true, "nat_1_1_address": "auto"}, {"address": "auto", "primary": false, "nat_1_1_address": null}]' \   --vpc.ipv4.ranges '[{"range": "/28"}, {"range": "10.11.12.0/24"}]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Linode interfaces](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-interfaces.md): This operation returns all interfaces assigned to a specific Linode. They list in the order they were created. On the Linode, interfaces also list in this order, and are typically named `eth0`, `eth1`, `eth2`, and so on. The MAC address is the most reliable method to identify an interface. The Linode needs to use `interfaces` and not configuration profile interfaces. Run [Get account settings](/reference/get-account-settings) to see if Linode interfaces are supported.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes interfaces-list $linodeId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List a Linode's network interface history](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-interface-history.md): **Beta** Lists the history of network interfaces by version for a Linode. This operation is for Linode interfaces, and not for legacy configuration profile interfaces.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-history-list $linodeId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Linode interface settings](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-interface-settings.md): Lists a Linode's interface settings, including Network Helper and default route settings. This operation is for Linode interfaces, not for legacy configuration profile interfaces.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linode:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes interfaces-settings-list $linodeId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update Linode interface settings](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-linode-interface-settings.md): Updates Network Helper and default route settings on the Linode. Before enabling or disabling Network Helper, you need to power off the Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  _Public interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-settings-update $linodeId \   --network_helper true \   --default_route.ipv4_interface_id 4527 \   --default_route.ipv6_interface_id 4541 `  _VLAN interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-settings-update $linodeId \   --network_helper true `  _VPC interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-settings-update $linodeId \   --network_helper true \   --default_route.ipv4_interface_id 5527 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Linode interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-interface.md): Returns an interface assigned to a specific Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-view $linodeId $interfaceId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a Linode interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-linode-interface.md): Update the configuration of a Linode interface.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  _Public interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-update $linodeId $interfaceId \   --default_route.ipv4 true \   --default_route.ipv6 false \   --public.ipv4.addresses '[{"address": "192.0.2.141", "primary": true}, {"address": "auto", "primary": false}]' \   --public.ipv6.ranges '[{"range": "2001:0db8"::1/64"}, {"range": "/64"}]' `  _VLAN interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-update $linodeId $interfaceId \   --vlan.vlan_label my-vlan \   --vlan.ipam_address 192.168.2.2/24 `  _VPC interface_  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-update $linodeId $interfaceId \   --default_route.ipv4 true \   --vpc.subnet_id 321 \   --vpc.ipv4.addresses '[{"address": "10.0.0.1", "primary": true, "nat_1_1_address": "auto"}, {"address": "auto", "primary": false, "nat_1_1_address": null}]' \   --vpc.ipv4.ranges '[{"range": "/28"}, {"range": "10.11.12.0/24"}]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a Linode interface](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-linode-interface.md): Deletes a Linode interface on a specific Linode. Consider these points when deleting a Linode interface:  - You can't delete an active interface. First, you need to shut down the associated Linode.  - You can't delete a Linode interface from a Linode that has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set for it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-delete $linodeId $interfaceId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Linode interface firewalls](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-interface-firewalls.md): Lists firewalls assigned to an interface.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes interface-firewalls-list $linodeId $interfaceId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Upgrade to Linode interfaces](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-upgrade-linode-interfaces.md): Automatically upgrades all legacy config interfaces of a single configuration profile to Linode interfaces. A Linode interface is directly associated with the Linode, rather than being tied to a configuration profile.  Firewalls are applied to the Linode interface, not directly to the Linode itself.  > ❗️ This upgrade is irreversible > > Once you upgrade a Linode to use Linode interfaces, you can't use legacy config interfaces. This means you can no longer use the Linode with any Linode products that require private IPs, such as NodeBalancer. You can use `dry_run` to preview the upgrade.  Before upgrading interfaces, you can check the new Linode interface configuration by performing a dry run, where you set `dry_run` to `true` or omit it. A `dry_run` runs the upgrade logic and returns a JSON representation of what the interface configuration will be after the upgrade without committing any changes.  When you run this operation with `dry_run` set to `false`, the following occurs:   - It creates matching interfaces on the Linode based on the interfaces present on the `config_id`.   - All firewalls are removed from the Linode. Any firewalls that were originally attached to the Linode are now applied to the public and VPC interfaces. Firewalls are not applied to VLAN interfaces. If the Linode has no firewalls attached, then default firewalls are not used.   - If no legacy config interfaces are defined (`legacy_config`) in the `config_id`, a public interface is created using the public IPv4 assigned to the Linode. The same is the case if the Linode has no config defined.   - For public interfaces, the Linode's current MAC address and SLAAC address are conserved. The MAC address won't change.   - It deletes all legacy config interfaces from all configs.   - It returns the list of interfaces for the Linode.  Requirements:   - The `config_id` for the legacy config interfaces can't use a public interface private IPv4 address.   - The Linode needs a MAC address in the database if it's IPv6 enabled. If it doesn't, an error message tells you what to do.   - The Linode must be in a region that supports Linode interfaces. Run [Get a region](/reference/get-region).   - Your account must allow creation of Linodes with Linode interfaces, run [Get account settings](/reference/get-account-settings).   - If the Linode has a user with a non-standard username, it can't be upgraded.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes interfaces-upgrade $linodeId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Allocate an IPv4 address](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-add-linode-ip.md): Allocates a public or private IPv4 address to a Linode. Public IP Addresses, after the one included with each Linode, incur an additional monthly charge. If you need an additional public IP Address you must request one - please [Open a support ticket](/reference/post-ticket). You may not add more than one private IPv4 address to a single Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes ip-add 123 \   --address 192.0.2.141 \   --type ipv4 \   --public true `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get networking information](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-ips.md): Returns networking information for a single Linode.  > 📘 > > If the target Linode has several configuration profiles that include a Virtual Private Cloud (VPC) interface, the response lists address information for all of the VPCs.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes ips-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Linode's IP address](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-ip.md): View information for a Linode's set of IPs, its Linode interfaces, and VPC IPs and ranges.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes ip-view 123456 192.0.2.164 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update an IP address's RDNS for a Linode](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-linode-ip.md): Updates the reverse DNS (RDNS) for a Linode's IP Address. This may be done for both IPv4 and IPv6 addresses.  Setting the RDNS to `null` for a public IPv4 address, resets it to the default `ip.linodeusercontent.com` RDNS value.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes ip-update 123 \   203.0.113.1 \   --rdns test.example.org `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete an IPv4 address](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-linode-ip.md): Deletes a public or private IPv4 address assigned to this Linode. Consider these points before deleting an IPv4 address:  - A delete will fail if the target address is the Linode's last remaining public IPv4 address, or if the address has a 1:1 NAT with an active VPC subnet address.  - You can't use this operation to delete an IPv4 address assigned to a Linode interface. Run the [update the Linode interface](/reference/put-linode-interface) operation instead.  - You can't delete an IPv4 address from a Linode that has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set for it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_linode` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes ip-delete 97.107.143.141 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get daily Linode statistics](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-stats.md): Returns CPU, IO, IPv4, and IPv6 statistics for your Linode for the past 24 hours.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_stats` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`
- [Get a month's Linode statistics](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-stats-by-year-month.md): Returns CPU, IO, IPv4, and IPv6 statistics for your Linode for a specific month. If the `{year}` and `{month}` are set to the current month, the API returns statistics for the past 30 days.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_monthly_stats` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`
- [Get this month's network transfer stats](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-transfer.md): Returns a Linode's network transfer pool statistics for the current month.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_linode_monthly_network_transfer_stats` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli linodes transfer-view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get monthly network transfer stats](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-linode-transfer-by-year-month.md): Returns a Linode's network transfer pool statistics for a specific month. If the `{year}` and `{month}` are set to the current month, the API returns statistics for the past 30 days.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `linodes:read_only`
- [List kernels](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-kernels.md): Lists available kernels on your Linodes.  > 👍 > > With the extensive list of available kernels, keep [pagination](/reference/pagination) controls in mind when managing responses to this operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli kernels list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a kernel](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-kernel.md): Returns information about a single kernel on a Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli kernels view latest-64bit `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Linode Kubernetes Engine (LKE)

- [Linode Kubernetes Engine (LKE) index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/linode-kubernetes-engine-lke/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a Kubernetes cluster](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-lke-cluster.md): Creates a Kubernetes cluster. The Kubernetes cluster will be created asynchronously. You can use the events system to determine when the Kubernetes cluster is ready to use. Please note that it often takes 2-5 minutes before the [Kubernetes API endpoints](/reference/get-lke-cluster-api-endpoints) and the [Kubeconfig file](/reference/get-lke-cluster-kubeconfig) for the new cluster are ready.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_lkecluster_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-create \   --label cluster12345 \   --region us-central \   --k8s_version 1.33 \   --apl_enabled false \   --control_plane.high_availability true \   --node_pools.type g6-standard-4 --node_pools.count 6 \   --node_pools.type g6-standard-8 --node_pools.count 3 \   --node_pools.autoscaler.enabled true \   --node_pools.autoscaler.max 12 \   --node_pools.autoscaler.min 3 \   --tags ecomm `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Kubernetes clusters](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-clusters.md): Lists current Kubernetes clusters available on your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke clusters-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Kubernetes cluster](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-cluster.md): Get a specific Cluster by ID.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-view 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a Kubernetes cluster](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-lke-cluster.md): Updates a Kubernetes cluster.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-update 12345 \   --label lkecluster54321 \   --control_plane.high_availability true \   --k8s_version 1.33 \   --tags ecomm \   --tags blog \   --tags prod \   --tags monitoring `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a Kubernetes cluster](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-lke-cluster.md): Deletes a cluster from your account.  > 🚧 > > Deleting a cluster is a destructive action and can't be undone.  When a cluster is deleted, all supporting Kubernetes services are removed from your account along with all Linodes in your cluster's node pools. You must have `read_write` permission to the cluster to initiate deletion.  Some additional services associated with your cluster remain active on your account. These services include NodeBalancers and Block Storage volumes. To avoid further charges, delete these services manually through Cloud Manager, the Linode CLI, or the Linode API.  Account administrators can configure a resource lock with the [Create a resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) operation to prevent a cluster from being deleted. They can also remove the lock with the [Delete a resource lock](/reference/delete-resource-lock) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Recycle cluster nodes](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-lke-cluster-recycle.md): Recycles all nodes in all pools of a designated Kubernetes Cluster. All Linodes within the Cluster will be deleted and replaced with new Linodes on a rolling basis, which may take several minutes. Replacement Nodes are installed with the latest available patch version for the Cluster's current Kubernetes minor release.  **Any local storage on deleted Linodes (such as `hostPath` and `emptyDir` volumes, or `local` PersistentVolumes) will be erased.**   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-nodes-recycle 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Regenerate a Kubernetes cluster](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-lke-cluster-regenerate.md): Regenerate the Kubeconfig file and/or the service account token for a Cluster.  This is a helper operation that allows performing both the [Delete a Kubeconfig](/reference/delete-lke-cluster-kubeconfig) and the [Delete a service token](/reference/delete-lke-service-token) operations with a single request.  When using this operation, at least one of `kubeconfig` or `servicetoken` is required.  > 📘 > > When regenerating a service account token, the cluster's control plane components and Linode CSI drivers are also restarted and configured with the new token. High availability clusters shouldn't experience any disruption, while standard clusters may experience brief control plane downtime while components are restarted.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke regenerate 12345 \     --kubeconfig true \     --servicetoken true `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get the control plane access control list](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-cluster-acl.md): Get a specific cluster's control plane access control List.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-acl-view 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update the control plane access control list](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-lke-cluster-acl.md): Updates a specific cluster's control plane access control list.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-acl-update 12345 \       --acl.enabled true \       --acl.addresses.ipv4 "203.0.113.1" \       --acl.addresses.ipv6 "2001:db8:1234:abcd::/64" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete the control plane access control list](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-lke-cluster-acl.md): Disables control plane access controls and deletes all rules. It has the same effect as calling `PUT` with an ACL JSON map value of `{"enabled": false}`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-acl-delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Kubernetes cluster dashboard URL](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-cluster-dashboard.md): **Deprecated** This operation has been deprecated. It previously returned the URL for the [Kubernetes Dashboard](https://github.com/kubernetes/dashboard) installed on your cluster, which enabled performance of administrative tasks through a web interface. See [Using the Kubernetes Dashboard on LKE](https://www.linode.com/docs/guides/using-the-kubernetes-dashboard-on-lke/).  > ❗️ LKE’s Kubernetes Dashboard has been decommissioned > > Following the upstream archival of the Kubernetes Dashboard project, Akamai Cloud decommissioned the LKE-hosted Kubernetes Dashboard. This change occurred in May 2026. The Kubernetes Dashboard is no longer available for existing LKE clusters and can't deploy to new clusters.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke cluster-dashboard-url 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Kubeconfig](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-cluster-kubeconfig.md): Get the Kubeconfig file for a Cluster. Please note that it often takes 2-5 minutes before the Kubeconfig file is ready after first [creating a new cluster](/reference/post-lke-cluster).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke kubeconfig-view 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a Kubeconfig](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-lke-cluster-kubeconfig.md): Delete and regenerate the Kubeconfig file for a Cluster.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke kubeconfig-delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a node](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-cluster-node.md): Returns the values for a specified node object.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke node-view 123456 12345-6aa78910bc `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a node](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-lke-cluster-node.md): Deletes a specific node from a node pool.  **Deleting a Node is a destructive action and cannot be undone.**  Deleting a node reduces the size of the node pool it belongs to.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke node-delete 12345 12345-6aa78910bc `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Recycle a node](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-lke-cluster-node-recycle.md): Recycles an individual Node in the designated Kubernetes Cluster. The Node will be deleted and replaced with a new Linode, which may take a few minutes. Replacement Nodes are installed with the latest available patch for the Cluster's Kubernetes Version.  **Any local storage on deleted Linodes (such as `hostPath` and `emptyDir` volumes, or `local` PersistentVolumes) will be erased.**   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke node-recycle 12345 12345-6aa78910bc `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a node pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-lke-cluster-pools.md): Creates a new node pool for the designated Kubernetes cluster.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke pool-create 12345 \   --type g6-standard-4 \   --count 6 \   --tags example-tag \   --autoscaler.enabled true \   --autoscaler.max 12 \   --autoscaler.min 3 \   --labels '{ "example.com/my-app":"team1" }' \   --taints.effect "NoSchedule" \   --taints.key "example.com/my-app" \   --taints.value "teamA" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List node pools](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-cluster-pools.md): Returns all active node pools on a Kubernetes cluster.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke pools-list 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a node pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-node-pool.md): Get a specific node pool.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke pool-view 12345 456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a node pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-lke-node-pool.md): Updates a node pool's count, labels, taints, and autoscaler configuration.  Linodes are created or deleted to match changes to the node pool's count.  Specifying labels or taints on update overwrites any previous values, and updates existing nodes with the new values without a recycle.  **Any local storage on deleted Linodes (such as `hostPath` and `emptyDir` volumes, or `local` PersistentVolumes) will be erased.**   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke pool-update 12345 456 \   --count 6 \   --autoscaler.enabled true \   --autoscaler.max 12 \   --autoscaler.min 3 \   --labels '{ "example.com/my-app":"team1", "env":"staging" }' \   --taints.effect "NoSchedule" \   --taints.key "example.com/my-app" \   --taints.value "teamA" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a node pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-lke-node-pool.md): Delete a specific node pool from a Kubernetes cluster.  **Deleting a node pool is a destructive action and can't be undone.**  Deleting a node pool deletes all Linodes within that pool.  Account administrators can configure a resource lock with the [Create a resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) operation to prevent a node pool from being deleted. They can also remove the lock with the [Delete a resource lock](/reference/delete-resource-lock) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke pool-delete 12345 456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Recycle a node pool](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-lke-cluster-pool-recycle.md): Recycles a node pool for the designated Kubernetes cluster. All Linodes within the node pool will be deleted and replaced with new Linodes on a rolling basis, which may take several minutes. Replacement nodes are installed with the latest available patch for the cluster's Kubernetes version.  **Any local storage on deleted Linodes (such as `hostPath` and `emptyDir` volumes, or `local` PersistentVolumes) will be erased.**   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke pool-recycle 12345 456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a service token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-lke-service-token.md): Delete and regenerate the service account token for a Cluster.  > 📘 > > When you regenerate a service account token, the cluster's control plane components and Linode CSI drivers are also restarted and configured with the new token. High availability clusters shouldn't experience any disruption, while standard clusters may experience brief control plane downtime while components are restarted.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke service-token-delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List LKE Kubernetes versions (any tier)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-tiers-versions.md): List LKE Kubernetes versions available for deployment to a Kubernetes cluster (any tier).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke tiered-versions-list standard `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an LKE Kubernetes version (any tier)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-tiers-version.md): View an LKE Kubernetes version available for deployment to a Kubernetes cluster (any tier)   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke tiered-version-view standard 1.31 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List LKE Kubernetes versions (non-enterprise)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-versions.md): List LKE Kubernetes versions available for deployment to a standard-tier Kubernetes cluster.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke versions-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an LKE Kubernetes version (non-enterprise)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-version.md): View an LKE Kubernetes version available for deployment to a standard tier Kubernetes cluster.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke version-view 1.31 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Kubernetes types](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-types.md): Returns Kubernetes types and prices, including any region-specific rates.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke types `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Kubernetes API endpoints](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-lke-cluster-api-endpoints.md): List the Kubernetes API server endpoints for this cluster. Please note that it often takes 2-5 minutes before the endpoint is ready after first [creating a new cluster](/reference/post-lke-cluster).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `lkecluster_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `lke:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli lke api-endpoints-list 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Linode StackScripts

- [Linode StackScripts index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/linode-stackscripts/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a StackScript](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-add-stack-script.md): Creates a StackScript in your Account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_stackscript_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `stackscripts:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli stackscripts create \   --label a-stackscript \   --description "This StackScript install and configures MySQL" \   --images "linode/debian13" \   --images "linode/debian13" \   --is_public true \   --rev_note "Set up MySQL" \   --script '#!/bin/bash' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List StackScripts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-stack-scripts.md): If the request is not authenticated, only public StackScripts are returned.  For more information on StackScripts, please read our [StackScripts documentation](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/stackscripts).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `Public StackScripts are Internet facing, and Private StackScripts require the stackscript_viewer role` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `stackscripts:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli stackscripts list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a StackScript](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-stack-script.md): Returns all of the information about a specified StackScript, including the contents of the script.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `stackscript_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `stackscripts:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli stackscripts view 10079 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a StackScript](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-stack-script.md): Updates a StackScript.  **Once a StackScript is made public, it cannot be made private.**   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `stackscript_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `stackscripts:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli stackscripts update 10079 \   --label a-stackscript \   --description "This StackScript installs \     and configures MySQL" \   --images "linode/debian13" \   --images "linode/debian13" \   --is_public true \   --rev_note "Set up MySQL" \   --script '#!/bin/bash' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a StackScript](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-stack-script.md): Deletes a private StackScript. You can't delete a public StackScript.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `stackscript_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `stackscripts:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli stackscripts delete 10079 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Longview

- [Longview index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/longview/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a Longview client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-longview-client.md): Creates a Longview Client.  This Client will not begin monitoring the status of your server until you configure the Longview Client application on your Linode using the returning `install_code` and `api_key`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_longview_creator` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `longview:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview create \   --label client789 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Longview clients](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-longview-clients.md): Returns a paginated list of Longview Clients you have access to. Longview Client is used to monitor stats on your Linode with the help of the Longview Client application.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `longview:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Longview client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-longview-client.md): Returns a single Longview Client you can access.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `longview:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview view 789 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a Longview client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-longview-client.md): Updates a Longview Client.  This cannot update how it monitors your server; use the Longview Client application on your Linode for monitoring configuration.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `longview:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview update 789 \   --label client789 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a Longview client](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-longview-client.md): Deletes a Longview Client from your Account.  **All information stored for this client will be lost.**  This _does not_ uninstall the Longview Client application for your Linode - you must do that manually.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `longview:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview delete 789 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Longview plan](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-longview-plan.md): Get the details of your current Longview plan. If your current plan is Longview Free, this operation returns an empty set `{}`.  To update your subscription plan, send a request to [Update a Longview plan](/reference/put-longview-plan).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_viewer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `longview:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview plan-view `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a Longview plan](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-longview-plan.md): Update your Longview plan to that of the given subscription ID. If the updated plan is Longview Free, this operation returns an empty set `{}`.  You can send a request to the [List Longview subscriptions](/reference/get-longview-subscriptions) operation to receive the details, including `id`'s, of each plan.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `account_longview_subscription_admin` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `longview:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview plan-update --longview_subscription longview-10 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Longview subscriptions](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-longview-subscriptions.md): Returns a paginated list of available Longview subscriptions.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_viewer`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview subscriptions-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a Longview subscription](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-longview-subscription.md): Get a specific Longview subscription plan.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_admin`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview subscription-view `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Longview types](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-longview-types.md): Returns Longview types and prices, including any region-specific rates.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs these roles assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Roles: `longview_viewer`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli longview types `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Maintenance

- [Maintenance index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/maintenance/llms.txt): full category index
- [List maintenance policies](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-maintenance-policies.md): List all available maintenance policies that can be applied to your Linodes.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli maintenance policies-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Managed

- [Managed index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/managed/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a managed contact](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-managed-contact.md): Creates a managed contact. When investigating or resolving an issue with a service monitor, Akamai Support may reach out to this contact.  This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli managed contact-create \   --name "John Doe" \   --email "john.doe@example.org" \   --phone.primary "123-456-7890" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List managed contacts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-managed-contacts.md): Returns a paginated list of managed contacts on your account.  This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli managed contacts-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a managed contact](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-managed-contact.md): Returns a single managed contact.  This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli managed contact-view 567 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a managed contact](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-managed-contact.md): Updates information for a managed contact.  This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli managed contact-update 567 \   --name "John Doe" \   --email "john.doe@example.org" \   --phone.primary "123-456-7890" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a managed contact](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-managed-contact.md): Deletes a managed contact.  This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli managed contact-delete 567 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a managed credential](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-managed-credential.md): Creates a managed credential to store usernames and passwords for applications running on your Linode. Akamai Support can use these credentials to access your applications when investigating or resolving issues.  This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. - [Create a managed service](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-managed-service.md): Creates a service monitor. This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. - [List managed stats](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-managed-stats.md): Returns a list of metrics for the Linodes on your account in arrays of coordinates (_x_-axis and _y_-axis data points). You can use these data points to plot your own graph visualizations. These metrics reflect the last 24 hours of combined usage across all managed Linodes on your account, giving you a high-level snapshot of data for the following:  - CPU - Disk I/O - Swap usage - Network in - Network out  This operation can only be accessed by the unrestricted users of an account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. ## API Reference: Marketplace

- [Marketplace index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/marketplace/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a Marketplace third-party referral](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-marketplace-contact.md): **Beta** Allows you as an Akamai customer to use our Marketplace application to request access to a third-party product offered by one of our qualified partners. Once the request is received, Akamai reaches out on your behalf for this access. Once approved, the qualified partner will contact you with details on accessing the product.  > 📘 > > For available product information, access [Cloud Manager](https://cloud.linode.com), select **COMPUTE** > **Partner Referrals (BETA)** and review the product cards. Store the value listed in the partner logo as your `partner_name` and the full name of the product, listed below the logo, as your `product_name`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli marketplace contacts-create \   --product_name DBaaS \   --partner_name Akamai \   --name "John Doe" \   --email "john.doe@example.org" \   --country_code US \   --phone_country_code +1 \   --phone 555 555 1234 \   --tc_consent_given true `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
