[Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_nodebalancer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers view 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a NodeBalancer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-node-balancer.md): Updates information about a NodeBalancer you can access.  > 🚧 > > You can configure UDP on the same NodeBalancer that also uses TCP, HTTP, or HTTPS, but only when managing it through the API. If UDP is configured and you make changes to the TCP, HTTP or HTTPS settings in Cloud Manager, the existing UDP configuration will be overwritten. This is because Cloud Manager doesn't currently support UDP.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_nodebalancer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers update 12345 \   --label balancer12345 \   --client_conn_throttle 0 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a NodeBalancer](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-node-balancer.md): Deletes a NodeBalancer.  **This is a destructive action and cannot be undone.**  A NodeBalancer can't be deleted if it has a `cannot_delete` or a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` lock. Only account administrators can remove locks using the [Delete a resource lock](/reference/delete-resource-lock) operation.  Deleting a NodeBalancer also deletes all associated configurations and nodes. However, the backend servers represented by these nodes are not changed or removed.  When you delete a NodeBalancer, you lose access to the ephemeral IP addresses assigned to it. When a NodeBalancer is deleted, any assigned reserved IP remains on your account in an unassigned state, preserving the address for future use. Conversely, ephemeral IPs are released and can't be recovered.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_nodebalancer` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List NodeBalancer types](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-types.md): Returns NodeBalancer types and prices, including any region-specific rates.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers types `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a config](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-node-balancer-config.md): Creates a NodeBalancer configuration, which allows the NodeBalancer to accept traffic on a new port. You will need to add NodeBalancer nodes to the new configuration before it can actually serve requests.  > 🚧 > > You can configure UDP on the same NodeBalancer that also uses TCP, HTTP, or HTTPS, but only when managing it through the API. If UDP is configured and you make changes to the TCP, HTTP or HTTPS settings in Cloud Manager, the existing UDP configuration will be overwritten. This is because Cloud Manager doesn't currently support UDP.  After creating a NodeBalancer configuration, account administrators can run the [Create a resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) operation to prevent accidentally deleting configurations.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_nodebalancer_config` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  _HTTPS_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-create 12345 \   --port 443 \   --protocol https \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness http_cookie \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --check_passive true \   --proxy_protocol "none" \   --ssl_cert "-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----               CERTIFICATE_INFORMATION               -----END CERTIFICATE-----" \   --ssl_key "-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----              PRIVATE_KEY_INFORMATION              -----END PRIVATE KEY-----" \   --cipher_suite recommended `  _UDP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-create 12345 \   --port 80 \   --protocol udp \   --algorithm ring_hash \   --stickiness session \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --udp_check_port 80 `  _TCP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-create 12345 \   --port 80 \   --protocol tcp \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness none \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --proxy_protocol "v2" `  _HTTP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-create 12345 \   --port 440 \   --protocol http \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness http_cookie \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List configs](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-configs.md): Returns a paginated list of NodeBalancer Configs associated with this NodeBalancer. NodeBalancer Configs represent individual ports that this NodeBalancer will accept traffic on, one Config per port.  For example, if you wanted to accept standard HTTP traffic, you would need a Config listening on port 80.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_nodebalancer_config` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers configs-list 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a config](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-config.md): Returns configuration information for a single port of this NodeBalancer.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_nodebalancer_config` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-view \   12345 4567 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a config](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-node-balancer-config.md): Updates the configuration for a single port on a NodeBalancer.  > 🚧 > > You can configure UDP on the same NodeBalancer that also uses TCP, HTTP, or HTTPS, but only when managing it through the API. If UDP is configured and you make changes to the TCP, HTTP or HTTPS settings in Cloud Manager, the existing UDP configuration will be overwritten. This is because Cloud Manager doesn't currently support UDP.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_nodebalancer_config` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  _HTTPS_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-update \   12345 4567 \   --port 443 \   --protocol https \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness http_cookie \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --check_passive true \   --proxy_protocol "none" \   --ssl_cert "-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----               CERTIFICATE_INFORMATION               -----END CERTIFICATE-----" \   --ssl_key "-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----              PRIVATE_KEY_INFORMATION              -----END PRIVATE KEY-----" \   --cipher_suite recommended `  _UDP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-update \   12345 4567 \   --port 80 \   --protocol udp \   --algorithm ring_hash \   --stickiness session \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --udp_check_port 80 `  _TCP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-update \   12345 4567 \   --port 80 \   --protocol tcp \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness none \   --stickiness http_cookie \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --proxy_protocol "v2" `  _HTTP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-update \   12345 4567 \   --port 440 \   --protocol http \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness http_cookie \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a config](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-node-balancer-config.md): Deletes the configuration for a port of this NodeBalancer.  **This can't be undone.**  A NodeBalancer configuration can't be deleted if it has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` lock. Only account administrators can remove locks using the [Delete a resource lock](/reference/delete-resource-lock) operation.  Once completed, this NodeBalancer will no longer respond to requests on the given port. This also deletes all associated NodeBalancerNodes, but the Linodes they were routing traffic to will be unchanged and will not be removed.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_nodebalancer_config` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-delete \   12345 4567 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Rebuild a config](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-rebuild-node-balancer-config.md): Rebuilds a NodeBalancer config and its nodes that you have permission to modify.  Use this operation to update a NodeBalancer's config and nodes with a single request.  You can't rebuild a NodeBalancer config if it has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` lock. Only account administrators can remove locks using the [Delete a resource lock](/reference/delete-resource-lock) operation.  > 🚧 > > You can configure UDP on the same NodeBalancer that also uses TCP, HTTP, or HTTPS, but only when managing it through the API. If UDP is configured and you make changes to the TCP, HTTP or HTTPS settings in Cloud Manager, the existing UDP configuration will be overwritten. This is because Cloud Manager doesn't currently support UDP.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `rebuild_nodebalancer_config` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  _HTTPS_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-rebuild \   12345 4567 \   --port 443 \   --protocol https \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness http_cookie \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --check_passive true \   --proxy_protocol "none" \   --ssl_cert "-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----               CERTIFICATE_INFORMATION               -----END CERTIFICATE-----" \   --ssl_key "-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----              PRIVATE_KEY_INFORMATION              -----END PRIVATE KEY-----" \   --cipher_suite recommended \   --nodes.label "node1" --nodes.address "192.168.210.120:80" --nodes.mode "accept" --nodes.weight 50 \   --nodes '[{"address":"192.168.210.122:80","label":"node2","weight":50,"mode":"accept"}]' \   --nodes '[{"address":"10.0.0.45:80","label":"vpc-node","weight":10,"mode":"accept","subnet_id:1"}]' `  _UDP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-rebuild \    12345 4567 \    --port 80 \    --protocol udp \    --algorithm ring_hash \    --udp_check_port 80 \    --nodes.label "node1" --nodes.address "192.168.210.120:80" --nodes.mode "accept" --nodes.weight 50 \    --nodes '[{"address":"192.168.210.122:80","label":"node2","weight":50}]' \    --nodes '[{"address":"10.0.0.45:80","label":"vpc-node","weight":10,"mode":"accept","subnet_id:1"}]' `  _TCP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-rebuild \   12345 4567 \   --port 80 \   --protocol tcp \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness none \   --proxy_protocol "v2"   --nodes.label "node1" --nodes.address "192.168.210.120:80" --nodes.mode "accept" --nodes.weight 50 \   --nodes '[{"address":"192.168.210.122:80","label":"node2","weight":50,"mode":"accept"}]' \   --nodes '[{"address":"10.0.0.45:80","label":"vpc-node","weight":10,"mode":"accept","subnet_id:1"}]' `  _HTTP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers config-rebuild \   12345 4567 \   --port 440 \   --protocol http \   --algorithm roundrobin \   --stickiness none \   --check http_body \   --check_interval 90 \   --check_timeout 10 \   --check_attempts 3 \   --check_path "/test" \   --check_body "it works" \   --nodes.label "node1" --nodes.address "192.168.210.120:80" --nodes.mode "accept" --nodes.weight 50 \   --nodes '[{"address":"192.168.210.122:80","label":"node2","weight":50,"mode":"accept"}]' \   --nodes '[{"address":"10.0.0.45:80","label":"vpc-node","weight":10,"mode":"accept","subnet_id:1"}]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a node](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-node-balancer-node.md): Creates a backend node that can receive traffic for the NodeBalancer configuration. Requests are routed to backend nodes on the specified port based on their `status`. The configurable fields for the backend node depend on the chosen protocol and whether the node is located within a Linode VPC.  After creating a NodeBalancer backend node, account administrators can add a lock to prevent accidental node deletion by using the [Create a resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) operation.  > 🚧 > > You can configure UDP on the same NodeBalancer that also uses TCP, HTTP, or HTTPS, but only when managing it through the API. If UDP is configured and you make changes to the TCP, HTTP or HTTPS settings in Cloud Manager, the existing UDP configuration will be overwritten. This is because Cloud Manager doesn't currently support UDP.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_nodebalancer_config_node` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  _TCP, HTTP, HTTPS_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers node-create \   12345 4567 \   --address 10.0.0.45:80 \   --label node54321 \   --weight 50 \   --mode accept \   --subnet_id 1 `  _UDP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers node-create \   12345 4567 \   --address 192.168.210.120:80 \   --label node54321 \   --weight 50 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List nodes](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-config-nodes.md): Returns a paginated list of NodeBalancer nodes associated with this Config. These are the backends that will be sent traffic for this port.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_nodebalancer_config_nodes` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers nodes-list 12345 4567 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a NodeBalancer's node](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-node.md): Returns information about a single Node, a backend for this NodeBalancer's configured port.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_nodebalancer_config_node` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers node-view 12345 4567 54321 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a node](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-node-balancer-node.md): Updates information about a node, a backend for this NodeBalancer's configured port. The fields available for the backend node depend on the configuration's protocol, and if the backend node is in a VPC.  > 🚧 > > You can configure UDP on the same NodeBalancer that also uses TCP, HTTP, or HTTPS, but only when managing it through the API. If UDP is configured and you make changes to the TCP, HTTP or HTTPS settings in Cloud Manager, the existing UDP configuration will be overwritten. This is because Cloud Manager doesn't currently support UDP.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_nodebalancer_config_node` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  _TCP, HTTP, HTTPS_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers node-update \   12345 4567 54321 \   --address 192.168.210.120:80 \   --label node54321 \   --weight 50 \   --mode accept `  _UDP_  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers node-create \   12345 4567 \   --address 192.168.210.120:80 \   --label node54321 \   --weight 50 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a NodeBalancer's node](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-node-balancer-config-node.md): Deletes a node from a configuration. This backend will no longer receive traffic for the configured port of this NodeBalancer.  A NodeBalancer backend node can't be deleted if it has a `cannot_delete_with_subresources` lock. Only account administrators can remove locks using the [Delete a resource lock](/reference/delete-resource-lock) operation.  This does not change or remove the Linode whose address was used in the creation of this node.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_nodebalancer_config_node` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers node-delete \   12345 4567 54321 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List NodeBalancer firewalls](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-firewalls.md): View information for Firewalls assigned to this NodeBalancer.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_nodebalancer_firewalls` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers firewalls $nodeBalancerId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a NodeBalancer's firewalls](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-node-balancer-firewalls.md): Replace the current list of assigned firewalls with a new list, or provide an empty list to remove all firewalls from this NodeBalancer.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_nodebalancer_firewalls` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers firewalls-update 12345 \   --firewall_ids '[1234, 4567]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get NodeBalancer statistics](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-stats.md): Returns detailed statistics about the requested NodeBalancer.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_nodebalancer_statistics` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`
- [List VPC configurations](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-vpcs.md): Returns a paginated list of VPC configurations for the NodeBalancer.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_nodebalancer_vpc_configs` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers vpcs-list 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a VPC configuration](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-node-balancer-vpc-config.md): Returns a single VPC configuration for the NodeBalancer ID.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_nodebalancer_vpc_config` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `nodebalancers:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli nodebalancers vpcs-view \   12345 7 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Object Storage

- [Object Storage index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/object-storage/llms.txt): full category index
- [List Object Storage types](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-types.md): Returns Object Storage types and prices, including any region-specific rates.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage types `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get Object Storage transfer data](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-transfer.md): The amount of outbound data transfer used by your account's Object Storage buckets. Object Storage adds 1 terabyte of outbound data transfer to your data transfer pool. See the [Pricing](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/object-storage-pricing#network-transfer) guide for details on Object Storage transfer quotas.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage transfer-view `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Cancel Object Storage](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-cancel-object-storage.md): Cancel Object Storage on an Account.  > 🚧 > > This removes all buckets and their contents from your Account. This data is irretrievable once removed.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage cancel `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create an Object Storage access key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-object-storage-keys.md): Generates a new Object Storage access key and an associated secret key. The secret key is only displayed when the access key is generated and cannot be viewed again. A successful request triggers an `obj_access_key_create` [event](/reference/get-events).  > 📘 > > - Accounts with negative balances can't access this operation. > > - This operation has specific [rate limits](/reference/rate-limits#specific-operation-rate-limits).  **Unlimited vs. limited access keys**  An unlimited access key grants full access to all of your buckets in each region you name, using the `regions` array. A limited access key lets you name specific buckets where you need to manage content, using the `bucket_access` array.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer workflows for both an [unlimited access key](/reference/create-an-unlimited-access-key) and a [limited access key](/reference/create-a-limited-access-key).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage keys-create \   --label "my-object-storage-key" \   --bucket_access '[{"region": "ap-south", "bucket_name": "bucket-example-1", "permissions": "read_write" }]' `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Object Storage access keys](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-keys.md): Returns a paginated list of Object Storage access keys for authentication.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage keys-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an Object Storage access key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-key.md): Returns a single Object Storage access key.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage keys-view \   --keyId 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update an Object Storage access key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-object-storage-key.md): Updates an Object Storage access key on your account. A successful request triggers an `obj_access_key_update` [event](/reference/get-events).  > 📘 > > This operation has specific [rate limits](/reference/rate-limits#specific-operation-rate-limits).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage keys-update \   --keyId 12345   --label "my-object-storage-key" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Revoke an Object Storage access key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-object-storage-key.md): Revokes an Object Storage access key. Once revoked, the access key can no longer be used by third-party clients. A successful request triggers an `obj_access_key_delete` [event](/reference/get-events).  > 📘 > > This operation has specific [rate limits](/reference/rate-limits#specific-operation-rate-limits).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage keys-delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create an Object Storage bucket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-object-storage-bucket.md): Creates an Object Storage bucket in the specified data center ([region](/reference/get-regions)). If the bucket already exists on your account, this operation returns a 200 response with that bucket as if the API just created it.  > 📘 > > - Accounts with negative balances can't access this operation. > > - The API still supports the `clusterId` equivalent (`us-west-1`) when setting a `region` for a new bucket, but you should use the `regionId` (`us-west`) instead. > > - This operation has specific [rate limits](/reference/rate-limits#specific-operation-rate-limits).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`
- [List Object Storage buckets](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-buckets.md): Returns a paginated list of all Object Storage buckets available in your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`
- [Get an Object Storage bucket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-bucket.md): Returns a single Object Storage bucket.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`
- [List Object Storage buckets per region](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-bucketin-cluster.md): Returns a list of buckets on your account, in the specified region.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`
- [Remove an Object Storage bucket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-object-storage-bucket.md): Removes a single bucket.  > 📘 > > - You need to remove all objects from a bucket before you can delete it. While you can delete a bucket using the [CLI](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/using-s3cmd-with-object-storage#delete-a-bucket), this operation fails if the bucket contains too many objects. The best way to empty large buckets is to configure lifecycle policies. Set a policy to remove all objects, wait a day or more for the system to remove all objects, then delete the bucket. For more details, see [Lifecycle policies](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/lifecycle-policies).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`
- [Allow access to an Object Storage bucket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-object-storage-bucket-access.md): Apply basic cross-origin resource sharing (CORS) and [S3 canned access control list (ACL)](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/acl-overview.html#canned-acl) settings.  > 📘 > > - CORS is only supported with [legacy endpoints (E0 and E1)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/endpoint-types). > > - You can use the S3 API for more fine-grained control of the [CORS](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/enabling-cors-examples.html) or [S3 canned ACL](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/managing-acls.html) settings.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`
- [Get Object Storage bucket access](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-bucket-access.md): View the cross-origin resource sharing (CORS) and [S3 canned access control (ACL)](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/acl-overview.html#canned-acl) settings for a specific Object Storage bucket.  > 📘 > > You can use the S3 API to view more details on [CORS](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/enabling-cors-examples.html) or [S3 canned ACL](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/managing-acls.html) settings.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`
- [Update access to an Object Storage bucket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-storage-bucket-access.md): Update basic cross-origin resource sharing (CORS) and [S3 canned access control list (ACL)](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/acl-overview.html#canned-acl) settings.  > 📘 > > - CORS is only supported with [legacy endpoints (E0 and E1)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/endpoint-types). > > - You can use the S3 API for more fine-grained control of the [CORS](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/enabling-cors-examples.html) or [S3 canned ACL](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/managing-acls.html) settings.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`
- [List Object Storage bucket contents](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-bucket-content.md): Returns the contents of a bucket. The contents are paginated using a `marker`, that's the name of the last object on the previous page. Objects can also be filtered by `prefix` and `delimiter`. See [Filtering and sorting](/reference/filtering-and-sorting) for more information.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`
- [Create a URL for an object](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-object-storage-object-url.md): Creates a pre-signed URL to access a single object in a bucket. Use it to share, create, or delete objects by using the appropriate HTTP method in your request body's `method` parameter.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`
- [Get an Object Storage object ACL configuration](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-bucket-acl.md): View a specific object's access control list (ACL) settings. ACLs define who can access your buckets and objects and specify the level of access granted to those users. See [Define access and permissions using ACLs (Access Control Lists)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/define-access-and-permissions-using-acls-access-control-lists).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`
- [Update an object's ACL configuration](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-object-storage-bucket-acl.md): Update a specific object's access control list (ACL) settings. ACLs define who can access your buckets and objects, and specify the level of access granted to those users. See [Define access and permissions using ACLs (Access Control Lists)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/define-access-and-permissions-using-acls-access-control-lists).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`
- [Add a TLS/SSL certificate to a bucket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-object-storage-ssl.md): Upload a TLS/SSL certificate and private key to be served when you visit your Object Storage bucket via HTTPS. Your TLS/SSL certificate and private key are encrypted and stored at rest.  To replace an expired certificate, [delete your current certificates](/reference/delete-object-storage-ssl) and upload a new one.  > 📘 > > TLS/SSL certificates are only supported with [legacy endpoints (E0 and E1)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/endpoint-types).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage ssl-upload \   us-east-1 example-bucket \   --certificate "-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----                  CERTIFICATE_INFORMATION                  -----END CERTIFICATE-----" \   --private_key "-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----                  PRIVATE_KEY_INFORMATION                  -----END PRIVATE KEY-----" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a bucket's TLS/SSL certificate](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-ssl.md): Whether this bucket has a corresponding TLS/SSL certificate that was uploaded by a user on your account.   > 📘  >  > TLS/SSL certificates are only supported with [legacy endpoints (E0 and E1)](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/endpoint-types).   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage ssl-view \   us-east-1 example-bucket `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a bucket's TLS/SSL certificate](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-object-storage-ssl.md): Deletes this Object Storage bucket's user uploaded TLS/SSL certificate and private key.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage ssl-delete \   us-east-1 example-bucket `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List clusters](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-clusters.md): **Deprecated** Returns a paginated list of available Object Storage clusters.  > 🚧 > > This displays deprecated `clusterId` values that represent regions used with older versions of the API. It's maintained for backward compatibility. Run [List Object Storage endpoints](/reference/get-object-storage-endpoints) instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage clusters-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a cluster](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-cluster.md): **Deprecated** Returns a single Object Storage cluster.  > 🚧 > > This displays deprecated `clusterId` values that represent regions used with older versions of the API. It's maintained for backward compatibility. Run [Get a region](/reference/get-region) instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage clusters-view us-east-1 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Object Storage endpoints](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-endpoints.md): Returns a paginated list of all Object Storage [endpoints](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/endpoint-types) available in your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage endpoints `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Object Storage quotas](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-quotas.md): Returns the active Object Storage-related quotas applied to your account. For example, you may have a quota for the maximum number of buckets you can have on a single endpoint. The operation includes any quota overrides in the response.  > 📘 > > You can't combine parameters when [filtering](/reference/filtering-and-sorting) with this operation. Only a single filterable parameter can be used.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage quotas-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an Object Storage quota](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-quota.md): Returns information about a specific Object Storage-related quota on your account. The operation includes any quota overrides in the response.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage quota-view obj-objects-us-ord-1 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get Object Storage quota usage data](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-quota-usage.md): Returns usage data for a specific `objQuotaId`. This includes the maximum number of `objQuotaId` resources you can have for a single endpoint and the current usage for that resource.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage quota-usage-view obj-objects-us-ord-1 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List Object Storage global quotas](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-global-quotas.md): Returns the active Object Storage-related global quotas applied to your account, such as the maximum number of access keys. The operation includes any quota overrides in the response.  > 📘 > > You can't combine parameters when [filtering](/reference/filtering-and-sorting) with this operation. Only a single filterable parameter can be used.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage global-quotas-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an Object Storage global quota](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-global-quota.md): Returns a specific Object Storage-related global quota on your account. The operation includes any quota overrides in the response.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage global-quota-view keys `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get Object Storage global quota usage data](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-object-storage-global-quota-usage.md): Returns usage data for a specific global quota (`objGlobalQuotaId`). This includes the maximum number of resources permitted by the quota and your current usage for that resource.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `object_storage:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli object-storage global-quota-usage-view keys `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Placement groups

- [Placement groups index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/placement-groups/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a placement group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-placement-group.md): Creates a placement group on your account. Placement groups disperse your Linodes across physical machines (hosts) in one of our compute regions. Depending on your workload requirements, you may need your Linodes to follow specific strategies:  - **Grouped-together model**. You may want them placed close together to reduce latency between Linodes that are used for an application or workload. Affinity placement groups follow this model.  - **Spread-apart model**. You may want to disperse them across several hosts to support high availability, for example when required for failover. Anti-affinity placement groups follow this model.  To run this operation, your user needs the `add_linodes` [grant](/reference/get-user-grants). Talk to your local account administrator about grant management for your user.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer an example API [workflow](/reference/create-a-placement-group) you can follow to create a placement group and add Linodes to it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need permissions, based on the model you're using:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).      - Scopes:   `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli placement group-create \   --label PG_Miami_failover \   --region us-mia \   --placement_group_type "anti_affinity:local" \   --placement_group_policy strict `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List placement groups](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-placement-groups.md): Returns a paginated list of placement groups you have permission to view.  > 📘 > > Your user needs at least `read-only` [permission](/reference/get-user-grants) to all Linodes included in a placement group to view it. Placement groups that contain Linodes that you don't have this permission for are left out of the response. If all placement groups contain Linodes that lack this permission, the API returns a 403 error. Talk to your local account administrator about managing permissions.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need permissions, based on the model you're using:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).      - Scopes:   `placement:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli placement groups-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a placement group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-placement-group.md): View a specific placement group by ID.  > 📘 > > Your user needs at least `read-only` [permission](/reference/get-user-grants) to all Linodes included in a placement group to view it. If you don't have this access and call this operation, the API returns a 403 error. Talk to your local account administrator about managing permissions.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need permissions, based on the model you're using:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).      - Scopes:   `linodes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli placement group-view 528 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a placement group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-placement-group.md): Change the `label` for a specific placement group. This is the only value you can update. However, you can [add](/reference/post-group-add-linode) more Linodes or [remove](/reference/post-group-unassign) existing ones.  > 📘 > > To update a placement group, your [user grants](/reference/get-user-grants) need to include `read_write` permission to all of the Linodes in the group. Talk to your local account administrator about grant management for your user.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need permissions, based on the model you're using:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).      - Scopes:   `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli placement group-update 528 \   --label PG_Miami_failover_update `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a placement group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-placement-group.md): Deletes a placement group you have permission to `read_write`.  - Deleting a placement group can't be undone.  - All Linodes need to be [removed](/reference/post-group-unassign) before you can delete a placement group.  - If your placement group is non-compliant, you can't delete it. You need to wait for our help to bring it [compliant](https://www.linode.com/docs/products/compute/compute-instances/guides/placement-groups/#non-compliance-precedence).  \<`<LB>`>  > 📘 > > To run this operation, your user needs the `add_linodes` [grant](/reference/get-user-grants). Talk to your local account administrator about grant management for your user.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need permissions, based on the model you're using:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).      - Scopes:   `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli placement group-delete 528 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Assign a placement group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-group-add-linode.md): Add a Linode to your placement group. Check out our [example API workflow](/reference/create-a-placement-group) to create a placement group and add Linodes.  > 📘 > > Your user needs the `add_linodes` [grant](/reference/get-user-grants) and `read-write` [permission](/reference/get-user-grants) to the Linodes you want to add to the group. Talk to your local account administrator about grant and permissions management.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need permissions, based on the model you're using:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).      - Scopes:   `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli placement assign-linode 528 \   --linodes 123 456 \   --non-compliant true `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Unassign a placement group](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-group-unassign.md): Remove a Linode from your placement group.  > 📘 > > To run this operation, your user needs the `add_linodes` [grant](/reference/get-user-grants) and `read-write` [permission](/reference/get-user-grants) to the Linodes you want to remove from the group. Talk to your local account administrator about grant and permissions management.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need permissions, based on the model you're using:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).      - Scopes:   `linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli placement unassign-linode 528 \   --linode 123 456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Profile

- [Profile index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/profile/llms.txt): full category index
- [Get a profile](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-profile.md): Returns information about the current user. Use this to see who is acting in applications where more than one token is managed, such as a third-party OAuth application.  > 📘 > > A third-party OAuth application accessing a profile with this operation has full access to all aspects of that profile.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_profile`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile view `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a profile](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-profile.md): Update information in your profile.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, delegate users on a child account can't change their email address.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_profile` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile update \   --email example-user@gmail.com \   --timezone US/Eastern \   --email_notifications true \   --list_auth_method keys_only \   --two_factor_auth true \   --restricted false `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List authorized apps](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-profile-apps.md): This is a collection of OAuth apps that you've given access to your account, and includes the level of access granted.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_profile_apps` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile apps-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get an authorized app](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-profile-app.md): Returns information about a single app you've authorized to access your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_profile_app` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile app-view 1234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Revoke app access](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-profile-app.md): Expires this app token. This token can no longer be used to access your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `revoke_profile_app` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile app-delete 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List trusted devices](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-devices.md): Returns a paginated list of active trusted devices for your user. Browsers with an active Remember Me Session are logged into your account until the session expires or is revoked.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_profile_devices` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile devices-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a trusted device](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-trusted-device.md): Returns a single active trusted device for your user.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_profile_device` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile device-view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Revoke a trusted device](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-trusted-device.md): Revoke an active trusted device for your user.  Once a trusted device is revoked, this device will have to log in again before accessing your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `revoke_profile_device` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile device-revoke 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List grants](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-profile-grants.md): This returns a GrantsResponse describing what the acting User has been granted access to.  For unrestricted users, this will return a  204 and no body because unrestricted users have access to everything without grants.  This will not return information about entities you do not have access to.  This operation is useful when writing third-party OAuth applications to see what options you should present to the acting User.  For example, if they do not have `global.add_linodes`, you might not display a button to deploy a new Linode.  Any client may run this operation; no OAuth scopes are required.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_profile_grants`
- [List logins](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-profile-logins.md): Returns a collection of successful account logins from this user during the last 90 days.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_profile_logins` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile logins-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a profile's login](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-profile-login.md): Returns a login object displaying information about a successful account login from this user.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_profile_login` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile login-view 1234 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Send a phone number verification code](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-profile-phone-number.md): Send a one-time verification code via SMS message to the submitted phone number. Providing your phone number helps ensure you can securely access your Account in case other ways to connect are lost. Your phone number is only used to verify your identity by sending an SMS message. Standard carrier messaging fees may apply.  - By accessing this operation you are opting in to receive SMS messages. You can opt out of SMS messages by running the [Delete a phone number](/reference/delete-profile-phone-number) operation after your phone number is verified.  - Verification codes are valid for 10 minutes after they are sent.  - Subsequent requests made prior to code expiration result in sending the same code.  Once a verification code is received, verify your phone number with the [Verify a phone number](/reference/post-profile-phone-number-verify) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `send_profile_phone_number_verification_code` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli phone sms-code-send \   --iso-code US \   --phone-number 555-555-5555 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a phone number](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-profile-phone-number.md): Delete the verified phone number for the User making this request.  Use this operation to opt out of SMS messages for the requesting User after a phone number has been verified with the [Verify a phone number](/reference/post-profile-phone-number-verify) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_profile_phone_number` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli phone delete `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Verify a phone number](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-profile-phone-number-verify.md): Verify a phone number by confirming the one-time code received via SMS message after running the [Send a phone number verification code](/reference/post-profile-phone-number) operation.  - Verification codes are valid for 10 minutes after they are sent.  - Only the same User that made the verification code request can use that code with this operation.  Once completed, the verified phone number is assigned to the User making the request. To change the verified phone number for a User, first run the [Delete a phone number](/reference/delete-profile-phone-number) operation, then begin the verification process again with the [Send a phone number verification code](/reference/post-profile-phone-number) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `verify_profile_phone_number` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli phone verify \   --otp_code 123456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get user OAuth preferences](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-user-preferences.md): View a list of user preferences tied to the OAuth client that generated the token making the request. The user preferences endpoints allow consumers of the API to store arbitrary JSON data, such as a user's font size preference or preferred display name. User preferences are available for each OAuth client registered to your account, and as such an account can have multiple user preferences.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_user_preferences` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`
- [Update a user's OAuth preferences](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-user-preferences.md): Updates a user's preferences. These preferences are tied to the OAuth client that generated the token making the request. The user preferences endpoints allow consumers of the API to store arbitrary JSON data, such as a user's font size preference or preferred display name. An account may have multiple preferences. Preferences, and the pertaining request body, may contain any arbitrary JSON data that the user would like to store.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_user_preferences` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [Answer security questions](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-security-questions.md): Adds security question responses for your user. You need to use exactly three unique questions. Previous responses are overwritten if answered, or they're reset to `null` if unanswered.  > 📘 > > You need to answer these security questions before you can access the [Create a two factor secret](/reference/post-tfa-enable) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `answer_profile_security_questions` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [List security questions](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-security-questions.md): Returns a collection of security questions and their responses, if any, for your User Profile.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_profile_security_questions` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli security-questions list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Add an SSH key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-add-ssh-key.md): Adds an SSH Key to your Account profile.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_profile_ssh_key` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli sshkeys create \   --label "My SSH Key" \   --ssh_key "ssh-rsa AAAA_valid_public_ssh_key_123456785== user@their-computer" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List SSH keys](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-ssh-keys.md): Returns a collection of SSH Keys you've added to your Profile.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_profile_ssh_keys` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli sshkeys list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
