- [Get an SSH key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-ssh-key.md): Returns a single SSH Key object identified by `id` that you have access to view.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_profile_ssh_key` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli sshkeys view 42 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update an SSH key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-ssh-key.md): Updates an SSH Key. Only SSH key labels can be updated.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_profile_ssh_key` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli sshkeys update 42 \   --label "my laptop" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete an SSH key](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-ssh-key.md): Deletes an SSH Key you have access to.  > 📘 > > This operation only deletes a key's association from your profile. It doesn't remove it from any Linode or disk that was deployed with `authorized_keys`. You need to manually delete the key on the Linode or disk.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_profile_ssh_key` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli sshkeys delete 42 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Generate a secret key for two-factor authentication](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-tfa-enable.md): Generates a secret key for your user. To enable two-factor authentication (2FA), enter this secret into your third-party authenticator application. To complete the 2FA setup, use the [Enable two-factor authentication](/reference/post-tfa-confirm) operation to enter a one-time passcode (OTP) from your authenticator app. Once enabled, logins from untrusted computers are required to provide an OTP before they are successful.  Run the [Answer security questions](/reference/post-security-questions) operation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_profile_tfa_secret` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile tfa-enable `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Enable two-factor authentication](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-tfa-confirm.md): Confirms that you can successfully generate one-time codes. Once confirmed, 2FA is enabled on your account. Login attempts from untrusted computers will be required to provide a one-time code before they are successful.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `enable_profile_tfa` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile tfa-confirm \   --tfa_code 213456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Disable two-factor authentication](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-tfa-disable.md): Disables two-factor authentication (2FA) for your user. Once successful, login attempts from untrusted computers will only require a password. Keep 2FA enabled to protect your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `disable_profile_tfa` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile tfa-disable `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a personal access token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-personal-access-token.md): Creates a personal access token for your User. The raw token will be returned in the response, but will never be returned again afterward so be sure to take note of it. You may create a token with _at most_ the scopes of your current token. The created token will be able to access your account until the given expiry, or until it's revoked.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, delegate users can't use personal access tokens on child accounts. You need to [create a delegate user token](/reference/post-iam-delegation-profile-child-account-token) instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_profile_pat` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile token-create \   --scopes '*' \   --expiry '2018-01-01T13:46:32' \   --label linode-cli `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List personal access tokens](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-personal-access-tokens.md): Returns a paginated list of personal access tokens currently active for your user.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_profile_pats` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile tokens-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a personal access token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-personal-access-token.md): Returns a single personal access token.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_profile_pat` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile token-view 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a personal access token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-personal-access-token.md): Updates a Personal Access Token.  **Parent and child accounts**  In the context of the [parent and child accounts](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/parent-and-child-accounts-for-akamai-partners) feature, delegate users can't use personal access tokens on child accounts. You need to [create a delegate user token](/reference/post-iam-delegation-profile-child-account-token) instead.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_profile_pat` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile token-update 123 \   --label linode-cli `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Revoke a personal access token](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-personal-access-token.md): Revokes a personal access token. The token will be invalidated immediately, and requests using that token will fail with a 401. It is possible to revoke access to the token making the request to revoke a token, but keep in mind that doing so could lose you access to the api and require you to create a new token through some other means.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_profile_pat` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli profile token-delete 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Regions

- [Regions index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/regions/llms.txt): full category index
- [List regions](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-regions.md): Lists the regions available for Linode services. Not all services are guaranteed to be available in all regions.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli regions list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List regions' availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-regions-availability.md): Returns availability data for all regions.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli regions list-avail `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a region](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-region.md): Returns a single Region.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli regions view us-east `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a region's availability](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-region-availability.md): Returns availability data for a single Region.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli regions view-avail us-east `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Resource locking

- [Resource locking index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/resource-locking/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a resource lock](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-resource-lock.md): **Beta** Create a lock for a resource, to help prevent you from inadvertently deleting it or modifying some aspects of it. You can apply a lock to a single resource, or to a resource and all of its sub-resources. This operation can only be accessed by account [users](/reference/get-users) with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.  Once a resource has a lock, that resource (and its sub-resources, if applicable) can't be deleted, regardless of your user permission. You can unlock a resource by running the [Delete a resource lock](/reference/delete-resource-lock) operation for the specific lock.  > 👍 There's a tutorial > > We offer an example API [workflow](/reference/create-a-resource-lock-for-a-linode) you can follow to set up a resource lock on a Linode.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli resource-locks create \ --entity_id 8642531 \ --entity_type linode \ --lock_type cannot_delete \ `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List resource locks](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-resource-locks.md): **Beta** Returns all available locks and information on the resources they protect. This operation can only be accessed by account [users](/reference/get-users) with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli resource-locks list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a resource lock](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-resource-lock.md): **Beta** Returns a specific resource lock. This operation can only be accessed by account [users](/reference/get-users) with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli resource-locks view 25 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a resource lock](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-resource-lock.md): **Beta** Delete a specific resource lock. Once deleted, the resource it was assigned to is unlocked, and that resource can be deleted. This operation can only be accessed by account [users](/reference/get-users) with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.  > 📘 > > For specific delete instructions and requirements, see the relevant resource's delete operation documentation.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli resource-locks delete 25 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Support

- [Support index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/support/llms.txt): full category index
- [Open a support ticket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-ticket.md): Open a support ticket. A ticket can only target a single, specific entity. For example, for an issue with a specific Linode, open a ticket and target it using its `linode_id`. Leave all other entities out of the request or set them to `null`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tickets create \   --description "I'm having trouble setting the root password on my Linode. I tried following the instructions but something is not working and I'm not sure what I'm doing wrong. Can you please help me figure out how I can reset it?" \   --linode_id 123 \   --summary "Having trouble resetting root password on my Linode" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List support tickets](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-tickets.md): Returns a collection of all support tickets opened from your account. This includes tickets you've opened and tickets generated by Linode customer support regarding your account. Open tickets are returned first in the response.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tickets list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a support ticket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-ticket.md): Returns a specific support ticket under your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tickets view 11223344 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Close a support ticket](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-close-ticket.md): Closes a support ticket you have access to modify.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tickets close 11223344 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a support ticket attachment](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-ticket-attachment.md): Adds a file attachment to an open support ticket on your account. Use an attachment to help customer support resolve your ticket. The file attachment is submitted in the request as `multipart/form-data` type. Accepted file extensions include: `.gif`, `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.pjpg`, `.pjpeg`, `.tif`, `.tiff`, `.png`, `.pdf`, or `.txt`.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`
- [Create a reply](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-ticket-reply.md): Adds a reply to an existing support ticket.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tickets reply 11223344 \   --description "Thank you for your help. I was able to figure out what the problem was and I successfully reset my password. You guys are the best!" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List replies](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-ticket-replies.md): Returns a collection of replies to a support ticket on your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tickets replies 11223344 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Tags

- [Tags index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/tags/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a tag](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-tag.md): Creates a new tag and lets you optionally add it to specific objects. Tags are labels you can attach to objects in your account. Use them to specify and group attributes of objects that are relevant to you. Currently, you can add a tag to your `linodes`, your `nodebalancers`, your `reserved_ipv4_addresses`, the `domains` for your Linodes, and the `volumes` on your Linodes.  > 📘 > > - This operation can only be accessed by account users with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management. > > - If you don't add a tag to a supported object with this operation, you can use that object's update operation to later add the tag you created.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tags create \   --label 'example tag' \   --linodes 123 \   --linodes 456 \   --volumes 9082 \   --volumes 10003 \   --reserved_ipv4_addresses "192.0.2.141" `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List tags](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-tags.md): Returns a paginated list of tags you've [created](/reference/post-tag) on your account. This operation can only be accessed by account users with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tags list linode-cli tags ls `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List tagged objects](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-tagged-objects.md): Returns a paginated list of all objects you've tagged with the specified tag. The response includes a mixed collection of all object types. This operation can only be accessed by account users with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_only`
- [Delete a tag](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-tag.md): Deletes the tag and removes it from all tagged objects and reserved IPs. This doesn't delete the objects or reserved IPs. This operation can only be accessed by account users with _unrestricted_ access. Talk to your local account administrator about access management.  > 📘 > > Tags without any related objects are periodically cleaned up by the system.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `account:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli tags delete linode-cli tags rm `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: Volumes

- [Volumes index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/volumes/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-volume.md): Creates a [Block Storage](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-block-storage) volume on your account. Creating a new volume accrues additional charges on your account.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes create \   --label my-volume \   --size 20 \   --linode_id 12346 \   --no-defaults `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List volumes](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-volumes.md): Returns a paginated list of the Block Storage volumes that you have permission to view.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-volume.md): Get information about a specific Block Storage volume.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes view 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-volume.md): Update a specific Block Storage volume.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes update 12345 \   --label my_volume `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-volume.md): Delete a Block Storage volume. Some conditions apply when deleting a volume:  - Deleting a volume can't be undone.  - Deleting stops billing for the volume. You'll be billed for the time the volume was active during the current billing period.  - You can't delete a volume that's attached to a Linode currently being [migrated](/reference/post-migrate-linode-instance). You need to wait for the migration to complete.  - You can't delete a volume that has a `cannot_delete` or `cannot_delete_with_subresources` [resource lock](/reference/post-resource-lock) set for it.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes delete 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Attach a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-attach-volume.md): Attach a Block Storage volume to an existing Linode.  > 📘 > > - The volume and its target Linode need to exist in the same `region`. You can run the [List volumes](/reference/get-volumes) and [List Linodes](/reference/get-linode-instances) operations and review the `region` for each. > > - There's a maximum number of volumes that can be attached to a single Linode configuration. This number varies, based on the Linode's plan, with higher plan types supporting more volumes. The maximum follows a formula of the Linode plan's `total_ram / 1024`, with a minimum of 8 and a total maximum of 64 volumes. Rescue configurations support a maximum of 8.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `attach_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_write, linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes attach 12345 \   --linode_id 12346 \   --config_id 23456 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Clone a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-clone-volume.md): Target an existing Block Storage volume to create a new one. The resulting volume will be the same size and include the same data. A new volume will incur a charge on your account.  > 📘 > > Only a volume with a `status` of `active` can be cloned. Run the [List volumes](/reference/get-volumes) operation to view the `status` of each of your volumes.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `clone_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes clone 12345 \   --label my-volume `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Detach a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-detach-volume.md): Detach a Block Storage volume from a Linode. When you [delete](/reference/delete-linode-instance) a Linode, any volumes attached to it are automatically detached.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `detach_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_write, linodes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes detach 12345 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Resize a volume](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-resize-volume.md): Increase the size of an existing Block Storage volume on your account.  > 📘 > > - You can't decrease the size of an existing volume. > > - Only a volume with a `status` of `active` can be resized. Run the [List volumes](/reference/get-volumes) operation to view the `status` of each of your volumes.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `resize_volume` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `volumes:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes resize 12345 \   --size 30 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List volume types](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-volume-types.md): Returns Block Storage volume types and prices, including any region-specific rates.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. This operation is internet-facing. No permissions are required.   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli volumes types `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)

## API Reference: VPCs

- [VPCs index](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/vpcs/llms.txt): full category index
- [Create a VPC](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-vpc.md): Create a new VPC and optionally define new subnets within it. A successful request triggers a `vpc_create` event, and `subnet_create` events for any created VPC subnets.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_vpc` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `vpc:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs create \   --description "A description of my VPC." \   --label cool-vpc \   --region us-east \   --subnets.label cool-vpc-subnet \   --subnets.ipv4 10.0.1.0/24 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List VPCs](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-vpcs.md): Display all VPCs on your account.  The system automatically sorts the IPv4 ranges using standard Python network sorting logic, the primary sort is by network address, and then by prefix length.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_vpc`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a VPC](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-vpc.md): Get information about a single VPC.  The system automatically sorts the IPv4 ranges using standard Python network sorting logic, the primary sort is by network address, and then by prefix length.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_vpc`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs view $vpcId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a VPC](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-vpc.md): Update an existing VPC. A successful request triggers a `vpc_update` event.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_vpc` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `vpc:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs update $vpcId \   --description "A description of my VPC." \   --label myvpc `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a VPC](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-vpc.md): Delete a single VPC and all of its Subnets.  - A successful request triggers a `vpc_delete` event and `subnet_delete` events for each deleted VPC subnet. - All of the VPC's subnets need to be eligible for deletion. You need to delete all network interfaces each subnet is assigned to. If those interfaces are active, you need to shut down the associated Linodes first. If any subnet can't be deleted, it prevents the VPC and its subnets from being deleted. - You can't delete a VPC if a NodeBalancer or a managed database is attached to one of its subnets.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_vpc` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `vpc:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs delete $vpcId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List VPC IP addresses](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-vpcs-ips.md): Returns a paginated list of all VPC IP addresses and address ranges on your account.  > 📘 > > If a Linode has several configuration profiles that include a VPC interface, address information for all of them is listed in the response. Since VPCs can use the same address space, you may see duplicate IP addresses.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_vpcs_ip_addresses` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `ips:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs ips-all-list `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List a VPC's IP addresses](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-vpc-ips.md): Returns a paginated list of IP addresses for a single VPC.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `list_vpc_ip_addresses` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `ips:read_only`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs ip-list 123 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Create a VPC subnet](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/post-vpc-subnet.md): Create a VPC subnet within an existing VPC. A successful request triggers a `subnet_create` event.  After you create a VPC subnet, you can attach it to a Linode by assigning the subnet to one of the Linode's network interfaces.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `create_vpc_subnet` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `vpc:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs subnet-create $vpcId \   --label cool-vpc-subnet \   --ipv4 10.0.1.0/24 `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [List VPC subnets](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-vpc-subnets.md): Get information about all VPC Subnets associated with a VPC.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_vpc_subnet`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs subnets-list $vpcId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Get a VPC subnet](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-vpc-subnet.md): Get information about a single VPC Subnet.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `view_vpc_subnet`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs subnet-view $vpcId $vpcSubnetId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Update a VPC subnet](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/put-vpc-subnet.md): Update a VPC Subnet. A successful request triggers a `subnet_update` event.   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `update_vpc_subnet` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `vpc:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs subnet-update $vpcId \   --label cool-vpc-subnet `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
- [Delete a VPC subnet](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/delete-vpc-subnet.md): Delete a single VPC subnet. A successful request triggers a `subnet_delete` event.  > 📘 > > You need to delete all network interfaces, managed databases and NodeBalancers that this subnet is assigned to before you can delete it. If interfaces are active, the associated Linode needs to be shut down before they can be removed. For more information see: > > - [Delete a Linode interface](/reference/delete-linode-interface) and [Delete a configuration profile interface](/reference/delete-linode-config-interface) > - [Delete a MySQL Managed Database](/reference/delete-databases-mysql-instance) and [Delete a PostgreSQL Managed Database](/reference/delete-databases-postgre-sql-instance) > - [Delete a NodeBalancer](/reference/delete-node-balancer)   **Permissions and scopes**  To call this operation, you need the following:  - **Identity and access permissions**. Your user needs a role with these permissions assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/identity-access-cm-available-roles).   - Permissions: `delete_vpc_subnet` - **OAuth scopes**. Your user needs these scopes assigned. [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/linode-api/reference/get-started#oauth).    - Scopes: `vpc:read_write`   **CLI**  `shell linode-cli vpcs subnet-delete $vpcId $vpcSubnetId `  [Learn more](https://techdocs.akamai.com/cloud-computing/docs/getting-started-with-the-linode-cli)
